# Framework Evolution Ledger

**Purpose:** single authoritative record of every framework change made
during C12F development and the closure passes that followed. Each row lets a
reader answer *"why was this framework behavior changed, what evidence
motivated it, and what did that change affect?"* without terminal-log
archaeology.

Timestamps and hashes are recovered from the C12F run's `manifest.json` events
and this session's file writes. Where exact historical metadata is
unavailable it is marked `UNKNOWN`.

Fields per row:
`change_id · timestamp · run_context · problem · evidence · root_cause ·
change_type · files_changed · behavior_before · behavior_after ·
scientific_compute_changed · existing_sci_artifacts_changed · c12f_affected ·
authorization · tests · test_result · backward_compat · prospective_only ·
status`

---

## FE-001 — Bounded-evidence adapter for `uncertainty_report`
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-21T ~05:20Z (from C12F manifest.json `code_revision_reanchored` events, task #395)
- **run_context:** C12F Stage-9 iter008
- **problem:** Judge packet for Stage-9 exposed only top-level `uncertainty_report.json` keys, not criterion-relevant values. Judges returned `value_read=null` and REVISE.
- **evidence:** iter007 Stage-9 J1 hit JUDGE_INVALID_BLOCKER on 3/3 deterministic-contradiction attempts (from manifest).
- **root_cause:** evidence-serialization gap between authoritative artifact and Judge packet.
- **change_type:** evidence-readability
- **files_changed:** `runtimes/pydantic_ai/bounded_evidence.py` (uncertainty_report adapter)
- **behavior_before:** Judge saw field names only; no calibration/aggregate/per-frame values.
- **behavior_after:** bounded-evidence adapter surfaces calibration status, seeds, aggregate σ_F, per-frame summary from the pinned artifact.
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no
- **c12f_affected:** no (byte-identical Stage-9 artifacts through iter008)
- **authorization:** operator directive to fix Judge-facing evidence-serialization gap
- **tests:** `tests/test_stage9_uncertainty_evidence_surfacing.py`
- **test_result:** PASS (task #396)
- **backward_compat:** additive; older packets unaffected
- **prospective_only:** no
- **status:** VERIFIED

## FE-003 — Versioned Stage-10 gate spec v1→v2 (C2 → C2a/C2b/C2c)

- **evidence:** iter009 Stage-10 REVISE with `INVALID_JUDGE_OUTPUT` route triggered; the criterion itself lacked a decidable rule.

- **behavior_after:** C2a (composition scope, deterministic), C2b (pre-approved deployment point identity, deterministic), C2c (ensemble-aware pressure semantics with NOT_EVALUABLE ≠ FAIL).

- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no
- **c12f_affected:** no (Stage-10 artifacts byte-identical; v1 preserved verbatim)

## FE-004 — NVE unit-convention audit + executor conversion fix
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-21T ~08:45Z (session task 2b post-Stage-10 close)
- **run_context:** post-Stage-10 closure preparing Stage-11
- **problem:** `compute_nve_drift` returns meV/atom/ns; validation_profile threshold declared in meV/atom/ps. Pre-existing executor emitted raw ns-value against a ps-declared criterion → 1000× stricter than intended (over-strict, not lax).
- **evidence:** direct code inspection of `validation/structure_dynamics.py:139-158`; unit arithmetic derivation.
- **root_cause:** unit-convention mismatch at emission time.
- **change_type:** executor/physics
- **files_changed:** `runtimes/pydantic_ai/executors.py::_exec_build_physical_validation_report` (÷1000 conversion at emit)
- **behavior_before:** emit "meV/atom/ns" numeric compared as-is against 1.0 threshold declared in "meV/atom/ps".
- **behavior_after:** compute in meV/atom/ns then ÷1000 → emit meV/atom/ps; threshold value 1.0 unchanged; both raw and converted values recorded in details.
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no (no MD rerun)
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no
- **c12f_affected:** no (Stage-11 not yet gated in C12F)
- **authorization:** aligns emit to declared unit; not a threshold change (per operator directive)
- **tests:** `tests/test_nve_drift_unit_convention.py` (15 tests including synthetic ±0.5 / ±2.0 / boundary)
- **test_result:** PASS
- **backward_compat:** minor — R27 physical_validation_report if re-adjudicated under this executor would show numerically different values; historical R27 gate PASSes are unaffected (records byte-preserved).
- **prospective_only:** partial
- **status:** VERIFIED

## FE-006 — Framework V2 scientific-adequacy layer (typed contracts)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-21 session step 2a (governance-only closure pass)
- **run_context:** post-C12F closure pass
- **problem:** the framework had no typed carrier for a scientific-adequacy layer distinct from procedural validity, so a procedural PASS was silently sufficient to advance deployment even when the Student was inadequate. This was the root cause of C12F's evaluation acceptance.
- **evidence:** the read-only Stage-7–11 audit conducted earlier this session, which showed Stage-8 gate criteria were entirely procedural (no numerical fidelity threshold).
- **root_cause:** absence of a typed scientific-adequacy contract layer.
- **change_type:** scientific-policy
- **files_changed:** NEW `framework_v2/scientific_adequacy.py` (~620 lines); NEW `tests/test_framework_v2_scientific_adequacy.py` (21 tests including 6-parametrized C12F immutability guards)
- **behavior_before:** no typed scientific-adequacy contract; no fail-closed rule for missing thresholds.
- **behavior_after:** typed `AdequacyCriterion`, `EvaluationAdequacyPolicyV2`, `DeploymentScopeContractV2`, `StatePreparationPolicy`, `PhysicalValidationPolicyV2`, `UncertaintyPolicyV2`, `ScientificQuestion`, `RootCauseDiagnosis`, plus pure adjudicators (`evaluate_adequacy`, `adjudicate_uncertainty`, `route_by_root_cause`, `criterion_passes`). All fail-closed on missing provenance / unfrozen thresholds / ambiguous scope.
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no
- **c12f_affected:** no
- **authorization:** operator governance-closure directive
- **tests:** 21 governance tests all PASS (see task #278 for prior V2 synthetic dry-run pattern)
- **test_result:** PASS
- **backward_compat:** additive; existing procedural contracts unchanged
- **prospective_only:** yes (a governance layer, not a rewriter of history)
- **status:** VERIFIED

## FE-010 — Stage-11 typed physical observable executor (Blocker 1 close)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-21 session final closure
- **run_context:** final closure pass
- **problem:** the typed `PhysicalValidationPolicyV2` had no consumer; species-specific coordination with frozen reference-derived cutoff and RDF first-peak/first-minimum position were unimplemented.
- **evidence:** current `compute_rdf` emits `(distances, partial_g_of_r)` where `partial_g_of_r[pair] → array`; `compute_coordination` uses `cutoffs.get(pair, cutoffs.get("default", 3.0))` — no species-specific per-frame distribution; no first-peak/first-minimum extraction anywhere.
- **root_cause:** typed observable emission never implemented.
- **change_type:** executor/physics
- **files_changed:** `validation/structure_dynamics.py` (NEW `compute_rdf_v2`, `compute_species_coordination`, `rdf_first_peak_and_minimum` helper); `runtimes/pydantic_ai/executors.py::_exec_build_physical_validation_report` (new typed-emission branch when `physical_validation_policy_v2_dict` is present; guard replaced with a policy-driven emission path); NEW `tests/test_typed_physical_observables.py`
- **behavior_before:** could not emit typed observables; fail-closed guard blocked execution under policy.
- **behavior_after:** consumes `PhysicalValidationPolicyV2` and emits exactly the requested observables with typed shape (rdf_peak_position vs rdf_peak_height, species-specific coordination with explicit frozen cutoff, coordination histogram, ensemble-aware density metadata). No silent fallback: policy without a corresponding computable observable → NOT_EVALUABLE per-observable, aggregated via existing scorer.
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no (analysis only)
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no
- **c12f_affected:** no
- **authorization:** operator final-closure directive
- **tests:** typed-observable regression suite
- **test_result:** PASS
- **backward_compat:** legacy `compute_rdf`/`compute_coordination` retained for existing callers
- **prospective_only:** no
- **status:** VERIFIED (this pass — see body of change)

## FE-015 — Canonical Judge-packet dispatch-surface migration (R1 close)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-21 session final closure PASS 1
- **run_context:** final closure pass R1
- **problem:** the framework's canonical Judge-task builder in `runtimes.pydantic_ai.cli._judge_task` did not carry the scientific-adequacy layer; a Judge task packet dispatched to `openai-responses:gpt-5.6-sol` (or any provider) would show procedural criteria only, so a Judge could procedurally PASS a scientifically inadequate stage.
- **evidence:** direct inspection of `_judge_task`; the `context` field carried only `review_lens`, `review_focus`, `stage`, `judge_evidence_packet`; no scientific-policy content_sha256, no `ScientificQuestion`, no adequacy verdict.
- **root_cause:** existing packet builder was procedural-only; the scientific block existed as a helper (`framework_v2/judge_packet_extension.py::build_scientific_extension_block`) but was not integrated into the dispatch surface.
- **change_type:** judge-contract
- **files_changed:** `runtimes/pydantic_ai/cli.py::_judge_task` (adds `context.scientific_adequacy_layer` + a PROCEDURAL-vs-SCIENTIFIC constraint) + new `_build_scientific_layer_for_stage` helper that also runs the deterministic adjudicator and includes the verdict summary in the packet
- **behavior_before:** Judges saw procedural criteria only; had no way to distinguish procedural PASS from scientific adequacy.
- **behavior_after:** every Judge task packet for `evaluation`/`uncertainty`/`deployment_md`/`physical_validation` now carries: generic `ScientificQuestion`, policy type + version, policy content_sha256, `preregistration_witness_ref`, in-scope domains / representative point, deterministic adequacy verdict + reasons, criterion-by-criterion observed values, PASS/FAIL/NOT_EVALUABLE state, plus an explicit PROCEDURAL-vs-SCIENTIFIC divider constraint. Three mutually-blind Judges receive identical scientific content (same policy content_sha256) with per-Judge lens still differing. Inert when no scientific policy bound (backward compat).
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no
- **c12f_affected:** no (C12F does not bind scientific_policies; the new layer is `scientific_layer_active: False` there)
- **authorization:** operator final closure directive PASS 1
- **tests:** `tests/test_r1_r2_final_closure.py::test_r1_*` (4 tests)
- **test_result:** PASS
- **backward_compat:** additive; historical Judge task packets are byte-preserved (the field is only present when a scientific policy is bound, which C12F never does)
- **prospective_only:** yes
- **status:** VERIFIED (this pass)

## FE-016 — True Controller-lifecycle synthetic 12-stage campaign (R2 close)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-21 session final closure PASS 2
- **run_context:** final closure pass R2
- **problem:** the 5-path E2E tests hit the adequacy scorer directly, not through the actual Controller lifecycle path across all 12 stages; a "clean synthetic PASS through the authoritative entry points" and fresh-run isolation from historical artifacts were not proven.

- **root_cause:** missing self-contained authoritative-lifecycle synthetic fixture.

- **behavior_after:** 8 synthetic-run tests (Paths A/B/C/D/E/F/G + isolation) using actual policy-binding + stage-entry + adequacy adjudicator + root-cause routing invariants. Fresh-run isolation test proves the scorer NEVER silently reaches into a sibling historical run: even with C12F artifacts present on disk, a synthetic run with no locally-supplied evidence adjudicates as NOT_EVALUABLE-blocked, not silently PASS.

## FE-021 — Semantic correction of Option-A independence criterion → B2 CLOSED without new DFT
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-21 session post-FE-020, following human operator scientific-independence semantics correction (verbatim: "the entire 11,424-frame dataset belonged to the Teacher dataset, therefore there are zero independent DFT frames — used an overly strict and scientifically incorrect notion of independence").
- **problem:** the FE-020 Option-A audit computed `eligible_independent_existing_DFT = raw_pool − (Teacher_train ∪ Teacher_val ∪ Teacher_test) = 0` using ARCHIVE-LEVEL independence ("outside the entire training-dataset archive"). The scientifically correct criterion is MODEL-FITTING and MODEL-SELECTION independence: a DFT-labeled frame is admissible for downstream Student evaluation/calibration iff it did not participate in Teacher gradient updates, optimizer/LR-scheduler decisions, EarlyStopping, best-checkpoint selection, or hyperparameter selection. The subtraction-based verdict wrongly disqualified Teacher TEST from that role.
- **evidence:** the recovered training config `teacher_run/tutorial_Allegro.yaml` (sha256 `a4c9ceacd5dc1c4baecefc5ae958d19589be1a3b49448d5c491e3f95e889b8b7`) — byte-identical to `.hydra/config.yaml` — declares `monitored_metric: val0_epoch/weighted_sum`. EarlyStopping (patience=20, min_delta=1e-3), ModelCheckpoint(filename=best, save_last=true), and ReduceLROnPlateau (factor=0.5, patience=5) ALL monitor that validation metric. `run: [train, test]` invokes the test dataloader exactly once at end of training. Training log `allegro_581561.out` shows exactly one final `Testing DataLoader 0: 286/286` pass — 286 batches × batch_size 4 = 1,142 test frames (partial last batch). No `test0_epoch/*` metric appears anywhere except that final summary; no callback monitors any test metric.
- **root_cause:** independence criterion misidentified as data-source-archive membership rather than fitting/selection participation.
- **corrected_verdict:** `TEACHER_TEST_INDEPENDENCE = VERIFIED_UNTOUCHED`. `TEACHER_VAL_INDEPENDENCE = NOT_INDEPENDENT`.
- **exact_reconstructed_split:** `torch.utils.data.random_split(dataset_of_len_11424, [0.8, 0.1, 0.1], generator=torch.Generator().manual_seed(123))` executed in the allegro_kisti env. Deterministic. Counts: **TRAIN 9,140 / VAL 1,142 / TEST 1,142** (total 11,424, disjoint, covers all). Subset order matches Python dict insertion order of the frozen training config `{train: 0.8, val: 0.1, test: 0.1}`.
- **reconstructed_index_files:**
  - `docs/FRESH_SOURCE_BUNDLE_ASSEMBLED_v1/teacher/provenance_recovery/teacher_train_indices.txt` sha256 `b1a00b048ccdfb0f85fe25398580e2db36aaf41994060f232795b336ef7bfcde`
  - `.../teacher_val_indices.txt` sha256 `2ca946d6549b7971afb60cea5e2069bfe57e0756bcb5629c951f9ac1716c3351`
  - `.../teacher_test_indices.txt` sha256 `724596b23a9702a52ade098024e5fb263f4334809f1a2733f81b827437f3168b`
  - `.../split_and_domain_report.json` sha256 `b8618996522a36b235c46e3d7936151ae577937a87bf7e02208327f7b2dffbcf`
- **teacher_test_primary_domain_coverage:** 8/8 PRIMARY_CLAIM domains covered. Counts: amorphous_bulk_SiO2=147, crystalline_SiO2=235, high_pressure_SiO2=93, liquid_or_melt_SiO2=34, surface_SiO2=86, oxygen_vacancy_SiO2=107, amorphous_SiOx_sub_stoichiometric=96, condensed_pure_Si_boundary=344. All 24 raw categories represented (min category count = 4 in `surfaces_max_AL`).
- **sufficiency_for_frozen_policies:**
  - **Stage-8 (EvaluationAdequacyPolicyV2)** — per-domain criteria are `student_vs_teacher::f_rmse ≤ 0.30 eV/Å` and `student_vs_teacher::e_rmse ≤ 25 meV/atom`. These use Teacher predictions (not DFT labels) as reference; Teacher TEST provides 1,142 structurally-diverse frames covering all 8 domains, disjoint from Student train/val (by preregistration). No DFT-independence dependency; ADEQUATE.
  - **Stage-9 (UncertaintyPolicyV2, required_status=calibrated_partial)** — needs an independent DFT-labeled population for split-conformal force-error calibration + a disjoint coverage-eval sub-population, with population-level binomial acceptance around 90% nominal coverage. Teacher TEST (1,142 DFT-labeled frames) can be preregistered-partitioned into, e.g., ~700 calibration-fit + ~442 calibration-eval, disjoint from each other AND from Student train/val (enforced by bundle access-partition manifest). At n=442, 95% binomial CI around 90% observed coverage ≈ ±2.8%. ADEQUATE.
- **B1_status:** **CLOSED** (unchanged from FE-020; identities now reconstructed to sha256-file level, previously only algorithmically re-materializable).
- **B2_status:** **CLOSED** (corrected from FE-020's `BLOCKED_NEW_DFT_REQUIRED`). Teacher TEST provides the admissible independent DFT-labeled population for Stage-9 calibration fit + eval. No new DFT compute required.
- **new_DFT_required:** NO. Option-B design document `OPTION_B_NEW_DFT_DESIGN.md` retained in the bundle directory as a HISTORICAL governance record; not the current path.

  - NEW `docs/FRESH_SOURCE_BUNDLE_ASSEMBLED_v1/teacher/provenance_recovery/CORRECTION_TEST_INDEPENDENCE.md` — the semantic-correction document (supersedes without erasing)

## FE-022 — Source-level-inputs-only readiness semantics (retract A/B/C preregistration proposal)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-21 session immediately after FE-021, per verbatim human correction: "Do NOT preregister or materialize a human-selected A/B/C frame partition. Do NOT use the previously proposed 60/25/15 split or the proposed exact per-domain A/B/C quotas. Those were advisory suggestions and are NOT approved campaign inputs. The user requires the final proof campaign to begin from raw/source-level inputs and make its own run-specific scientific execution decisions."
- **problem:** the FE-021 readiness line ended with "pending human-approved preregistered partition of the 1,142 Teacher TEST frames into Stage-8 protected-eval + Stage-9 calibration-fit + calibration-eval slots" and the earlier turn (this session) proposed a ~700/442 or ~240/500/300 illustrative slot sizing. Both moves pushed a run-specific scientific decision into bundle-level preregistration, violating the invariant that the fresh proof campaign must begin from source-level inputs and make its own prospective planning decisions.
- **corrected_semantics:** The source bundle proves ONLY the source-level invariants:
  - the untouched 1,142-frame Teacher TEST source population EXISTS;
  - it is PROTECTED (DFT labels access-partitioned; sanitized view has ZERO DFT truth);
  - its DFT labels cannot leak to early stages (Stage-1..7);
  - its TRAIN/VAL/TEST provenance is REPRODUCIBLE (deposited index files + deterministic-splitter procedure);
  - the fresh campaign has enough source-level METADATA (frozen policies, per-frame config_type + PRIMARY-domain mapping, structure counts, DFT-label reachability under access control) to perform its own prospective protected-population planning.
- **what_is_deliberately_NOT_in_the_bundle:** any preregistered A/B/C partition, any per-slot counts, any per-domain quotas, any allocation algorithm choice. `access_control/access_partitions.json` already correctly declares `PROTECTED_STAGE8_REFERENCE` / `UNCERTAINTY_CALIBRATION_FIT` / `UNCERTAINTY_CALIBRATION_EVAL` as **"Bundle-time this is a POLICY slot, not a materialized asset"** (verbatim); nothing else at bundle-time.
- **run_time_responsibility:** the fresh campaign itself must perform prospective protected-population planning consuming only: (1) the frozen scientific policy, (2) the reconstructed untouched Teacher TEST population, (3) domain/config metadata permitted by the policy, (4) statistical requirements for evaluation/calibration. It MUST NOT consume: C12F results, historical error maps, future Student predictions, future Student errors, committee disagreement, historical run-specific holdout assignments. After the planner assigns, freeze + hash the A/B/C manifests INSIDE the run, record in the run audit trail, prohibit subsequent mutation, and only then permit Student-dependent stages. If 1,142 frames are insufficient under the frozen policy, the run must FAIL CLOSED and request an appropriate RecoveryPlan / new-reference-data authorization.

## FE-023 — Generic protection-only structure-identity reference kind (separate PROTECTION vs EVALUATION reference)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-22 session (framework fix pass following the ffv1c diagnostic run)
- **run_context:** post-ffv1c framework evolution (fresh ffv1 campaign blocked at `teacher_baseline`/`acquisition`); LIVE runs ffv1/ffv1b/ffv1c preserved untouched as diagnostic evidence
- **problem:** the frozen protected-reference exclusion mechanism could not express an access-gated, identity-only protected population. Every protection path (`_protect_dataset`, `validate_protection_audit_report`, `_validate_acquisition_plan`) routes through `validate_reference_config`, whose only two kinds (`protected-existing-dft`, `recovered-original-holdout`) BOTH require a materialized **DFT-labeled** structures file. The fresh ffv1 reference is deliberately identity-only (DFT labels access-gated to Stage-8/9), so early-stage disjointness could not be run without exposing protected labels to Stages 1-7.
- **evidence:** ffv1c `manifest.json` `stage_execution_deferred` event at `teacher_baseline` with `ValueError: reference.kind 'protected-reconstructed-teacher-test' is not a recognized Teacher-vs-DFT reference kind`; deterministic trace that the same `validate_reference_config` call is reached again at the MANDATORY acquisition/dataset_split/teacher_labeling protection audits.
- **root_cause:** the protected-reference validator **conflated** two distinct concepts — a PROTECTION reference (early-stage disjointness; needs structure identity only: source-pool indices + geometry fingerprints) and an EVALUATION reference (Stage-8/9 DFT fidelity + calibration; legitimately carries DFT labels). Only the labeled EVALUATION representation existed, so protection could not be expressed without labels.
- **change_type:** access-control abstraction / framework plumbing (NOT a scientific-policy change)
- **files_changed:**
  - `validation/protected_reference.py` — added material-agnostic PROTECTION-ONLY kind `protected-structure-identity` (`reference_class=PROTECTION_ONLY_STRUCTURE_IDENTITY`, `status=IDENTITY_AVAILABLE_AND_PROTECTED`); validator `_validate_protection_only_structure_identity_reference` derives `protected_source_indices` from a newline-separated integer source-index file (sha256 + count verified) and `reference_fingerprints` from a geometry-only structures file (sha256 + `logical_frames` verified); fail-closed guard `_assert_frame_carries_no_label_truth` rejects any DFT/Teacher energy/force/stress/virial key in `atoms.info` / `atoms.arrays` / attached `calc.results` (key **presence only** — label values are never read); registered the new kind in `_REFERENCE_KIND_VALIDATORS`; added first-class capability classification (`_PROTECTION_ONLY_REFERENCE_KINDS`, `_EVALUATION_REFERENCE_KINDS`, `reference_kind_provides_dft_labels`, `reference_kind_of`, `assert_reference_is_protection_capable`, `assert_reference_provides_dft_labels`). Returns the SAME uniform protection dict as the existing kinds, so every existing consumer (`assert_source_indices_allowed` / `assert_dataset_geometry_disjoint` / `assert_parent_lineage_allowed` / `validate_protection_audit_report`) works unchanged.
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/executors.py` — `_exec_validate_teacher_reference` (the sole executor that reads the reference's OWN DFT labels, via `channel(..., "dft", "teacher", require_complete=True)`) now calls `assert_reference_provides_dft_labels(reference_yaml)` instead of `validate_reference_config(...)`, so a protection-only identity reference misrouted to the label-reading path FAILS CLOSED with a clear message rather than leaking into the label path.
- **behavior_before:** any protected reference had to materialize a DFT-labeled file; an identity-only access-gated reference was unrepresentable → early stages blocked.
- **behavior_after:** early-stage disjointness (acquisition/teacher_labeling/dataset_split/training) can be driven by a geometry-only + source-index identity reference that provably carries NO DFT/Teacher labels; the labeled EVALUATION path (`protected-existing-dft`/`recovered-original-holdout`) is unchanged and is the only path that reads labels; the two are separated as a first-class capability.
- **access_control_invariants_enforced:** (1) Stages 1-7 obtain protected structure identity (indices + fingerprints) but the validator never reads energy/force/stress; a protection manifest carrying label truth is rejected fail-closed. (2) A protection-only reference cannot be used as a Stage-8/9 evaluation reference (`assert_reference_provides_dft_labels` raises). (3) Tampering with the source-index or structures sha256 fails closed.
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no
- **frozen_policy_change_declaration:** NO file under `docs/FRESH_CAMPAIGN_FROZEN_POLICIES_v2_fresh_frozen_framework_validation/` was modified. EvaluationAdequacyPolicyV2 / UncertaintyPolicyV2 / DeploymentScopeContractV2 / StatePreparationPolicy / PhysicalValidationPolicyV2 / frozen thresholds / Teacher TEST identity all UNCHANGED. This is access-control plumbing only.
- **historical_run_immutability:** `runs/` shows zero modifications this pass — ffv1, ffv1b, ffv1c diagnostic runs (workflow/manifest/audit/approval/failure evidence) preserved byte-immutable; C12F canonical artifacts + FE-017 v1 / FE-018 v2 / FE-019 sanitized-pool bundles untouched.
- **c12f_affected:** no
- **authorization:** human operator (verbatim, this session): "A로 진행하세요." — Option A: fix the framework generically (no SiO2-only exception), separate PROTECTION vs EVALUATION reference, enforce Stage-1..7 no-DFT-label access, preserve LIVE runs, add tests A-H, re-verify closure + append ledger, then (only after verification) start a NEW fresh run.
- **tests:** `tests/test_protection_only_structure_identity_reference.py` (18 cases): A/B/C early-stage disjointness PASS via `validate_protection_audit_report` (parametrized acquisition/teacher_labeling/dataset_split); D fail-closed three ways (protected geometry in candidate, protected source index selected, protected parent lineage); E protection reference carrying a DFT label rejected + protection-only reference rejected on the evaluation-label path + executor-level fail-closed wiring; F existing DFT-labeled EVALUATION reference (`recovered-original-holdout`) still validates AND passes the label path; G capability classification + unknown-kind rejection regression; H tampered source-index/structures sha256 + count-mismatch + duplicate-geometry fail-closed; plus `write_protection_audit` end-to-end.
- **test_result:** PASS — 18/18 new; 29/29 combined with `test_recovered_holdout_reference.py` + `test_teacher_labeling_protection_audit.py`; full suite 1485 passed / 7 skipped with 5 PRE-EXISTING failures unrelated to this change (proven pre-existing by re-running them with this change stashed: `controller.py` frozen-file drift from earlier campaign work, 2 torchvision circular-import environment failures, `production_readiness` + `local_provider` fixture issues).
- **backward_compat:** additive — new kind + new helpers; the two existing reference kinds and every existing protection consumer are unchanged and still pass.
- **prospective_only:** yes (used by the next fresh run; existing runs untouched)
- **new_framework_revision:** protection-fix content hash `a5d81adf9b9c91e4a8db4d35cb64f80e67fdf0f6cf51b379c20455710fb4f452` (sha256 over `validation/protected_reference.py` + `runtimes/pydantic_ai/executors.py` + the new test file). Per-file sha256: `protected_reference.py=a0dc005af4a10dfec0f6efe7b4bfb7b3fb8286e0b032986d028ecf5b98dfa998`, `executors.py=e7b6d36f2a32e5641d0373ba50715b28d574ae9ba0930314c28826d13d537587`, `test=fd66a46270a95dd60d47eb957ab1726ebb93c1effe17583927d017311989c62c`.
- **status:** VERIFIED (this pass); framework ready for a NEW fresh run authoring a `kind: protected-structure-identity` reference from the deposited identity-only bundle (test_indices.txt + geometry-only structures) — do NOT resume ffv1c.

## FE-025 — Objective-conditioned AUTONOMOUS acquisition planner (`framework_v2/acquisition`) replacing the human-supplied 14-field AcquisitionPlan

- **timestamp:** 2026-08-22 session (FRAMEWORK EVOLUTION, executed OUTSIDE any live proof run; post-FE-024 R32 freeze)
- **run_context:** no live run touched — this is a framework-code capability addition authored between the R32 freeze and the next fresh proof campaign. LIVE runs ffv1/ffv1b/ffv1c + ffv2 + C12F preserved byte-immutable.
- **problem:** the acquisition stage required a human to author a low-level 14-field `AcquisitionPlan` (parent selection + `n_parents`/`n_per_structure`/`T_K`/`beta`/`sigma_range_A`/`cell_sigma`/`seed` + backend choice). Per the standing project finding "Stage-3 acquisition plan UNDERSPECIFIED", these knobs are NOT framework-derivable from frozen inputs today, so every fresh run stalled at a human boundary that mixed a genuine high-level scientific decision (target/claim-scope/compute-ceiling) with mechanical low-level recipe choices a reasoning agent can make under deterministic checks. This mirrors exactly the earlier Teacher-validation-planner evolution (FE teacher_validation_plan / cli auto-invocation), which moved the analogous low-level knobs to Agent-autonomy behind fail-closed validators.
- **root_cause:** there was no deterministic pipeline that (a) turns a high-level `CampaignObjective` + source/capability inventory into an admissible decision space, and (b) accepts an Agent-proposed recipe within that space under fail-closed validation. The only mechanism was a fully human-specified plan.
- **change_type:** framework capability addition (autonomous acquisition planning) — NOT a scientific-policy change.
- **files_changed (all NON-frozen; zero frozen files modified):**
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/` (NEW package) — deterministic pipeline contracts + logic: `contracts.py` (typed ContractBase evidence chain CampaignObjective → SourceAndCapabilityInventory → TargetRegimeModel → RegionResolution → CoverageGapAnalysis → AcquisitionStrategy → CandidateGenerationResult → CandidateSelectionResult → CanonicalLabelingRequest → AcquisitionPlanV2), `inventory.py`, `target_regime.py`, `region_resolution.py` (tiered metadata trust), `coverage_gap.py`, `strategy.py` (`select_strategy`), `generators/{base,local_perturbation,teacher_dynamics}.py` (BOTH backends behind one generic core), `selection.py`, `labeling.py`, `plan_assembly.py` (`build_legacy_projection`), `validators.py` (`validate_acquisition_plan_v2`), `__init__.py`.
  - `framework_v2/review_spec.py` — ACQUISITION `default_stage_review_specs()` entry now carries the `aq-objective-consistency` criterion (failure_code `dataset_coverage`), fail-closed against `workflow.recovery_taxonomy`.
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/acquisition_plan.py` (NEW) — typed `AcquisitionPlanProposal` + contextual fail-closed `validate_acquisition_plan_proposal` (+`AcquisitionPlanProposalValidationError`); mirrors `teacher_validation_plan`'s proposal/validator split. Binds the proposal to the exact coverage analysis via `coverage_gap_sha256`.
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/acquisition_planner.py` (NEW) — injected `AcquisitionPlanningProvider` Protocol (applies/build_context/realize); module-level `_PROVIDER=None`; `plan_acquisition_via_reasoning_roles(...)` is a HARD NO-OP (returns None, binds nothing) unless a provider applies; runtime `register_reasoning_output_model` registration of the proposal model (frozen `role_outputs.py` untouched); bounded semantic-correction retry (max_attempts=3) whose reasoning_validator runs BOTH `validate_acquisition_plan_proposal` (contextual) AND `provider.realize` + `validate_acquisition_plan_v2` (deterministic), feeding the rejection reason back into the next task; fails closed `EXIT_VALIDATION_REJECTED` on a non-legacy (dynamics-protocol) plan at binding.
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/tool_manifests.py` — added `propose_acquisition_plan` to `ORCHESTRATOR_BRIDGE_ACTIONS` (bridge-surface only; `ROLE_ALLOWED_ACTIONS`/`CAPABILITY_REGISTRY` for the 4 producer/analyst roles UNCHANGED, per the pinned architecture-freeze boundary test).
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/orchestrator_bridge.py` — added `_exec_propose_acquisition_plan` to `_BRIDGE_EXECUTORS`; it binds the already-`validate_acquisition_plan_v2`-validated legacy-projection plan through the FROZEN `RunController.bind_new_input(plan_path, copy=True)` — the same mechanism `initialize()` uses for a human-supplied plan; generates no candidates and never runs the Teacher.
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/cli.py` — added `mock_acquisition_response` param + automatic pre-campaign invocation of `plan_acquisition_via_reasoning_roles` in `_run_campaign_loop` (a hard no-op unless a provider is registered).
- **three binding design adjustments (verbatim human intent, all implemented):** (1) BOTH `LOCAL_PERTURBATION` AND `TEACHER_DRIVEN_MD` backends implemented through the same generic generator core; backend chosen autonomously by `select_strategy` (SiO2 does NOT necessarily use Teacher-MD). (2) Tiered metadata trust in `region_resolution.py`: no metadata → full DISCOVERED; metadata present → DECLARED + mandatory lightweight consistency audit; mismatch/suspicious/weak-provenance → ESCALATE to DISCOVERED. Metadata is evidence, not ground truth; material-agnostic + deterministically verifiable. (3) Separate evidence contracts by phase: INITIAL ACQUISITION (no trained Student) may use ONLY target-regime coverage gain / descriptor novelty+diversity / redundancy / region saturation / available-source coverage / generator yield+rejection diagnostics / optional compute ceiling — enforced by a fail-closed INITIAL-phase forbidden-token scan (no model uncertainty/EIG); MODEL-INFORMED REACQUISITION may additionally use calibrated uncertainty/disagreement/model-based EIG via a separate path.
- **access_control / fail-closed invariants enforced:** (a) nothing here binds or executes anything except the audited orchestrator bridge → FROZEN `RunController.bind_new_input`; producer/analyst executor surface untouched. (b) `validate_acquisition_plan_proposal` rejects: a stale/different `coverage_gap_sha256`, any `selected_parent_ids` not a subset of the deterministically-admissible pool, any producer attempt to OVERRIDE the deterministically-selected `strategy_kind`, missing required params, and any numeric param outside its caller-supplied admissible interval. (c) `validate_acquisition_plan_v2` re-checks evidence-chain SHA integrity, INITIAL forbidden-token scan, provenance separation, disjointness (PASS + zero overlaps + no DFT-label scores), execution-projection consistency (14 legacy fields; expected_output_count == n_parents*n_per_structure), and backend feasibility. (d) NO material-specific default or heuristic is invented in framework code — every bound/param comes from the caller's admissible decision space.
- **provider seam (opt-in, no SiO2 leakage):** the SiO2-specific `AcquisitionPlanningProvider` is INTENTIONALLY NOT implemented in framework code; the planner stays a hard no-op until the next fresh run registers a provider. No material-specific values enter the framework.
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no (no Teacher/DFT/MD executed this pass)
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no
- **frozen_policy_change_declaration:** NO file under `docs/FRESH_CAMPAIGN_FROZEN_POLICIES_v2_fresh_frozen_framework_validation/` was modified. EvaluationAdequacyPolicyV2 / UncertaintyPolicyV2 / DeploymentScopeContractV2 / StatePreparationPolicy / PhysicalValidationPolicyV2 / frozen thresholds / Teacher-TEST identity all UNCHANGED. This is a framework CODE capability addition only.

Plus `default_backend_probe` (enumerates shipped generators + per-env feasibility), `default_teacher_probe` (pins Teacher identity to the content of the run's OWN bound Teacher model; fails closed `NO_LABELING_TEACHER` if absent), `install_default_acquisition_provider`, and `maybe_install_default_acquisition_provider` (auto-install at run-campaign startup, removing the `_PROVIDER=None` hard no-op WITHOUT run-specific Python; returns None when a provider is already registered or the framework ships no plugin).

The bounded semantic-correction retry + fail-closed validators are unchanged.

Imports NOTHING from `provider.py`/`pydantic_ai_runtime.py` and constructs no client, so "no remote LLM/API call during replay" is guaranteed BY CONSTRUCTION. `run` recomputes (via the SAME shared functions `build_invocation` uses — `sha256_text`, `_input_hashes`, `ReadOnlyToolset.tool_manifest_sha256`) the instruction-prompt SHA, per-role input-artifact SHAs, and read-only tool-manifest SHA, checks provider/model/runtime identity, and (for Judge attempts) the recorded packet/decision SHAs; ANY mismatch raises `ReplayMismatch` (hard fail-closed). One recorded attempt per `run` in recorded order per `task_id` (mirrors Mock's one-responder-per-run), so a bounded-retry+correction sequence replays via successive `run` calls. The returned candidate is fed to the driver's EXISTING `validate_agent_response`, so the real deterministic validators + gate run unchanged. `load_provenance_records` / `from_provenance_dir` load a recorded exchange's `provenance/*.json` (skipping provider-exception records) in `(started_at, recorded_at, attempt_id)` order.

## FE-027 — OBJECTIVE_TO_EVIDENCE_SYNTHESIS_AUTONOMY: material-AGNOSTIC descriptor-space synthesis from raw structures (generic fallback descriptor provider + representation/regions/coverage/protocol from data+physics)

- **timestamp:** 2026-08-22 session (FRAMEWORK EVOLUTION, executed OUTSIDE any live proof run; post-FE-026).
- **run_context:** no live run touched — framework-code capability completion authored on top of FE-026. LIVE runs ffv1/ffv1b/ffv1c + ffv2 + C12F preserved byte-immutable; `runs/` unmodified.
- **problem:** FE-026 completed the autonomous acquisition MECHANISM but left its material-specific input — a `DescriptorSpaceEvidence` bundle — as a per-material `StructuralDescriptorProvider` a human still had to author (the VERIFIED SiO2 boundary at the close of FE-026: fresh generation param-bounds + per-region coverage-adequacy). That made a BRAND-NEW material `resolve_descriptor_provider`-fail-closed until someone wrote run-specific Python. The standing directive required distinguishing a TRUE high-level scientific-authorship boundary from a GENERIC scientific decision the framework had not yet automated, and closing the latter.
- **root_cause:** the descriptor space (which structural axes discriminate the pool), the target-relevant region structure, the two coverage axes, the acquisition sizing, and the generation-recipe bounds were all being treated as material-specific authored inputs, when in fact each is DERIVABLE from the run's own raw pool + versioned framework physics knobs — no material name/phase/cutoff constant is required to compute them.
- **change_type:** framework capability completion (material-agnostic descriptor-space synthesis + generic fallback provider) — NOT a scientific-policy change. NARROW completion ON TOP OF FE-026 (no redesign, no parallel reimplementation of the FE-026 materializer/plan-assembly/Judge/recovery surfaces, which are reused unchanged).
- **files_changed (all NON-frozen; zero frozen files modified):**
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/generic_representation.py` (NEW, P1) — builds a metadata-free raw-structure representation (species/positions/cell/pbc → generic continuous descriptor axes incl. `mean_min_neighbor_distance_A`) with a first-class comparative adequacy assessment; fails closed `REPRESENTATION_INSUFFICIENT` (typed `AcquisitionCapabilityGap`) when no admissible representation discriminates the pool — never prompts a human for a descriptor.
  - `framework_v2/representation_adequacy.py` (MODIFIED, P1; was FE-025-era `db6c7e5a…` → `f0c986c9…`) — additive support for the generic adequacy assessment consumed by the generic representation path.
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/generic_regions.py` (NEW, P2) — deterministic region DISCOVERY vs Agent-proposed target RELEVANCE (typed `RelevanceRole`) with a deterministic `validate_relevance_proposal` + `assemble_target_regime_model`; free-text objective → executable membership rule.
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/generic_coverage.py` (NEW, P3) — the two coverage axes + evidence-driven sizing: `compute_saturation`/`_axis_scales` (pool farthest-point marginal-novelty curve), `compute_target_regime_coverage_inputs`, `recommend_acquisition_sizing`, versioned `FrameworkSizingParams` (`saturation_threshold=0.8` etc.). `target_count` is an OUTPUT of the deterministic plateau analysis, never fabricated.
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/generic_protocol.py` (NEW, P4) — generic `ProtocolEnvelope` for both backends: LOCAL_PERTURBATION displacement bounded by a fraction of the pool's OWN nearest-neighbor spacing; TEACHER_DRIVEN_MD step-count bounded by the compute ceiling with absolute temperature presence-checked + recorded `unbounded_from_raw_structure`; output-admissibility bounds reject atomic overlap / over-strain; fails closed `PROTOCOL_ENVELOPE_UNGROUNDED` when no frame carries a computable NN distance. Bounds from DATA+PHYSICS, never a material constant.
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/generic_replay.py` (NEW, P5) — deterministic surfaces the repeated-live decision-stability benchmark drives.
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/generic_provider.py` (NEW, P1/P6) — `GenericStructuralDescriptorProvider` (`material_id="generic-raw-structure"`): `applies` broadly (any v2 campaign with a PRIMARY_DEPLOYMENT region + a schema-detectable raw-structure pool), and `build_descriptor_space_evidence` assembles the FULL material-agnostic `DescriptorSpaceEvidence` from P1 representation + P2 discovered regions + P3 pool saturation/novelty + P4 perturbation envelope. Every value DERIVED from the run's own raw pool; `metadata_present=False` routes the DISCOVERED region path.
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/descriptor_plugins.py` (MODIFIED; FE-026 `7c7138e8…` → `4702278d…`) — added the SEPARATE generic-fallback slot: `register_generic_descriptor_provider` / `generic_fallback_provider`, `resolve_descriptor_provider` now consults the fallback ONLY when zero specialized plugins apply (a specialized plugin that `applies` always wins; >1 specialized still fails closed AMBIGUOUS), and `clear_descriptor_providers` clears both slots.
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/builtin.py` (MODIFIED; FE-026 `6b85573d…` → `fd3f1fa7…`) — added `register_builtin_generic_fallback()` which lazily imports + registers `GenericStructuralDescriptorProvider` into the fallback slot, so the framework now ALWAYS ships a material-agnostic provider (heavy import stays lazy).

## FE-028 — EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION_AUTONOMY: configuration-acquisition vs labeling-population selection split, canonical existing-pool strategy/backend, autonomous labeling-population sizing, and DERIVED Teacher-dynamics capability

- **timestamp:** 2026-08-23 session (FRAMEWORK EVOLUTION, executed OUTSIDE any live proof run; post-FE-027).
- **run_context:** no live run touched — framework-code correction authored on top of FE-027. LIVE runs ffv1/ffv1b/ffv1c + ffv2 + ffv3 + ffv4b + C12F + all prior diagnostics preserved byte-immutable; `runs/` unmodified. The paused ffv4b run (`sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4b`, fail-closed at Stage-3 acquisition with `STRATEGY_UNDECIDABLE`/`FRESH_FE027_PATH_STILL_BLOCKED`) was NOT resumed or mutated; this correction is authored so the NEXT fresh run resolves the same state autonomously.
- **problem:** a saturated pool (`unsaturated_core_gaps == 0`) with an eligible existing population and canonical Teacher labeling still to be performed terminated the FE-027 autonomous path at `STRATEGY_UNDECIDABLE`. That conflated two distinct questions: "must NEW configurations be generated?" (answer: NO — `NEW_CONFIGURATION_GENERATION_NOT_REQUIRED`) versus "which admissible existing subset should be labeled?" (answer: an OUTPUT of deterministic scientific analysis). The framework had no canonical strategy for "select a representative existing subset for labeling," so a fully-decidable state was mis-reported as an irreducible gap.
- **root_cause:** (1) `select_strategy` had no branch for a saturated/eligible-pool state and fell through to `StrategyUndecidable`; (2) there was no `EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION` strategy/backend/projection in the canonical registry, so even a correct decision had nothing to realize; (3) labeling-population size was not derivable — it would have required a human `target_labeled_population` knob, exactly the low-level input the framework was designed to eliminate; (4) the production Teacher capability probe hardcoded `can_drive_dynamics=False`, which is incompatible with a generic `TEACHER_DRIVEN_MD` route and is not an actual inspection of the run's own bound Teacher.
- **change_type:** framework capability CORRECTION (strategy semantics + existing-pool backend/projection + autonomous labeling-population sizing + DERIVED Teacher-dynamics capability) — NOT a scientific-policy change. NARROW correction ON TOP OF FE-027; the FE-027 generic representation / RepresentationAdequacyEvidence / 3-Judge committee / Lens contracts / ReplayAgentRuntime / Teacher-first ValidationProfile / frozen scientific policies / 12-stage lifecycle are reused UNCHANGED and were NOT reopened.
- **files_changed (all NON-frozen; zero frozen files modified):**
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/strategy.py` (MODIFIED; sha `4912cdcb…`) — added `select_strategy` branch #1: `EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION` is chosen when it is admissible AND (`pool_covers_gaps` OR (`not gaps_require_new_configurations` AND `seed_structures_exist`)); rationale distinguishes "existing pool coverage closes unsaturated core gaps" from "no unsaturated core gaps (saturated source coverage): no new-configuration generation required; select a representative existing subset for canonical labeling." `StrategyUndecidable` is now raised ONLY when no admissible backend can close the gaps (a genuinely irreducible state), never for the normal saturated+eligible-pool+labeling-required outcome. The classic LOCAL_PERTURBATION (#2), TEACHER_DRIVEN_MD (#3, still gated on `inventory.teacher.can_drive_dynamics`), and STRUCTURE_GENERATION (#4) branches are preserved byte-for-byte in ordering/semantics.
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/contracts.py` (MODIFIED; sha `4d769b03…`) — `AcquisitionStrategyKind.EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION` enum member; `AcquisitionPlanV2.existing_pool_projection` as a THIRD mutually-exclusive projection under the existing `_exactly_one_projection` validator (`sum(...) == 1`); `ProtectedDisjointnessReport` (fail-closed `_pass_means_no_overlap`) and `CandidateSelectionResult` (`_selected_nonempty_and_disjoint`) for the existing-pool selection path; `TeacherCapabilityRecord(teacher_id, can_label, can_drive_dynamics, identity_sha256, rationale)` for the DERIVED capability.
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/generic_coverage.py` (MODIFIED; sha `29ad9215…`) — autonomous labeling-population sizing: `recommend_labeling_population_sizing(eligible_vectors, *, params, sizing_id, coverage_gap_sha256, protected_excluded_count=0, target_labeled_population=None, max_teacher_label_calls=None)` returns a typed `LabelingPopulationSizingEvidence`; size is an OUTPUT of an FPS marginal-novelty knee (`_fps_order_and_curve`, matching `selection.farthest_point_selection` exactly: raw Euclidean, `seed_index=0`, ties→lowest index) with a scientifically-defensible diminishing-benefit criterion. `target_labeled_population` / `max_teacher_label_calls` are OPTIONAL constraints (upper-bound clamp / ceiling), never required; the conservative fallback is the FULL eligible population (`fallback_full_population`). `FrameworkSizingParams` (`knee_novelty_fraction=0.1`, `saturation_threshold=0.8`, `min_frames_for_curve=3`, `batch_growth_factor=1.0`, `version="generic_sizing_v1"`). No material-specific counts; the LLM never invents N.

  - `framework_v2/acquisition/plan_assembly.py` (MODIFIED; sha `c588da0a…`) — `build_existing_pool_projection(pool_path, eligible_source_categories, selected_parent_structure_ids, selected_source_global_indices, labeling_population_sizing, selection_result, duplicate_handling)` assembles the typed existing-pool projection (selected global indices + parent ids + sizing evidence + selection result) into the `AcquisitionPlanV2` chain.
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/validators.py` (MODIFIED; sha `89c48d06…`) — `EXISTING_POOL_REQUIRED_FIELDS` + deterministic existing-pool projection validation (fields present, indices in range, disjointness PASS, sizing consistent) invoked before any binding.

## FE-029 — REPRESENTATION_SIZING_COMPATIBILITY: physically-correct periodic nearest-neighbour descriptor + missing-axis-tolerant labeling-population sizing (no frame dropping, no imputation)

- **timestamp:** 2026-08-24 session (FRAMEWORK EVOLUTION, executed OUTSIDE any live proof run; post-FE-028).
- **run_context:** no live run touched — framework-code correction authored on top of FE-028. LIVE runs ffv1/ffv1b/ffv1c + ffv2 + ffv3 + ffv4b + ffv4c + C12F + all prior diagnostics preserved byte-immutable; `runs/` unmodified. The fresh run `sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4c` (fail-closed BEFORE the costly Teacher/GPU boundary at `AcquisitionCapabilityGap(REPRESENTATION_INCOMPLETE)`, outcome RESOURCE_BLOCKED / exit 6, 2026-08-23T11:36:10Z) is retained IMMUTABLE as the FE-029 diagnostic run; it was NOT resumed or mutated. This correction is authored so the NEXT fresh run resolves the same state autonomously.
- **problem:** a fresh SiO2-x proof run drove past teacher-validation planning into autonomous acquisition planning with a saturated, eligible existing pool (the FE-028 `EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION` path) and fail-closed at `AcquisitionCapabilityGap(REPRESENTATION_INCOMPLETE)` on the FIRST eligible frame missing a chosen descriptor axis — it did NOT reach the genuine costly Teacher/GPU boundary. Trip frame: `silicon_crystalline_main#100`, a single-atom (1 Si) periodic primitive cell whose `mean_min_neighbor_distance_A` was reported uncomputable. Measured blast radius (read-only): 110 / 11424 pool frames are single-atom, ALL in category `silicon_crystalline_main`, ALL lacking the geometry axis.
- **root_cause:** a contract collision between two framework surfaces, plus a physically-wrong descriptor definition. (1) `_mean_min_neighbor_distance` returned `None` for any n<2-atom cell because it only considered distances to OTHER atoms under the minimum-image convention (`get_all_distances(mic=…)`, diagonal→inf) and did NOT consider an atom's own nearest PERIODIC image — but a one-atom periodic primitive cell has a perfectly well-defined nearest-neighbour distance (the shortest lattice translation), so the descriptor was spuriously undefined. (2) `compute_frame_features` deliberately TOLERATES uncomputable axes (emits them as evidence gaps, never imputed) and representation adequacy/discovery tolerate the sparse frames — but the FE-028 labeling-population-sizing path in `default_acquisition_provider.build_context` HARD-REQUIRED every eligible frame to carry ALL chosen descriptor axes to build the dense FPS matrix, and raised `REPRESENTATION_INCOMPLETE` on the first frame missing one. A fully-resolvable planning state was thus mis-reported as an irreducible capability gap before the costly boundary.
- **change_type:** framework capability CORRECTION (physically-correct periodic descriptor + representation/sizing missing-axis compatibility) — NOT a scientific-policy change. NARROW correction ON TOP OF FE-028; the FE-027 generic representation / RepresentationAdequacyEvidence / 3-Judge committee / Lens contracts / ReplayAgentRuntime / Teacher-first ValidationProfile / FE-028 `EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION` / autonomous sizing / Teacher capability derivation / frozen scientific policies / 12-stage lifecycle are reused UNCHANGED and were NOT reopened.
- **files_changed (all NON-frozen; zero frozen files modified):**
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/generic_representation.py` (MODIFIED; FE-027 `f60f1089…` → `9d015a63…`) —
    - NEW `_shortest_periodic_image_distance(atoms)`: the length of the shortest non-zero lattice translation over the PERIODIC directions, via a deterministic bounded `±3` integer-combination search over the periodic lattice vectors (`itertools.product` of `range(-3,4)` on periodic axes, `(0,)` on non-periodic axes; `combo @ cell`; smallest non-zero norm). Purely geometric — NO element, NO phase name, NO cutoff. Only PBC-flagged directions contribute, so partial PBC (e.g. a slab) is handled correctly. Returns `None` when no direction is periodic.
    - MODIFIED `_mean_min_neighbor_distance(atoms)`: the nearest neighbour of an atom is now the closest of (a) any OTHER atom under the minimum-image convention (`mic=periodic`) and (b) — for a periodic cell — the atom's own nearest periodic image via `_shortest_periodic_image_distance`. A one-atom periodic cell now yields a finite, physically-meaningful distance. Returns `None` ONLY when genuinely undefined: an isolated NON-periodic single atom (no other atom, no periodic image), which the caller records as an evidence gap, never imputed.
    - `compute_frame_features` unchanged in contract (n_atoms + max_species_fraction always; number_density + mean_min_neighbor_distance_A only when computable, never imputed) — but now emits the geometry axis for periodic single-atom frames.
    - NEW `SizingRepresentation` frozen dataclass (spec, adequacy, axes, reduced, per_axis_available_count).
    - NEW `audit_axis_availability(pool, continuous_variables)`: deterministic per-axis count of eligible frames carrying each axis; an axis is universally available iff its count equals `pool.total_frames`.
    - NEW `derive_admissible_sizing_representation(pool, *, candidate_spec, scope_contract, deployment_claim, id_prefix, region_classifier=None)`: (1) axis-availability audit over ALL eligible frames; (2) ordered, de-duplicated trial axis-sets, most-faithful first — the FULL candidate axes (added only if universally available), then the universally-available candidate subset, then `ALTERNATIVE_CONTINUOUS_VARIABLES ∩ availability`; (3) for each trial present on EVERY frame, build the representation + spec + a `RepresentationAdequacyEvidence(kind="discriminative_power", supports_adequacy = n_regimes>1)` and run `assess_representation_adequacy` (alternatives_considered = the candidate spec); accept the FIRST whose verdict is PASS and return it as `SizingRepresentation(reduced = axes != candidate axes)`; (4) if none passes, fail closed with typed `AcquisitionCapabilityGap(gap_kind="REPRESENTATION_INSUFFICIENT")`. Every accepted axis is present on every frame, so the dense sizing matrix contains ALL eligible frames — axis incompleteness NEVER erases a structure, an axis is NEVER silently removed, and NO value is ever imputed.
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/default_acquisition_provider.py` (MODIFIED; FE-028 `4a7203f6…` → `49f817b4…`) — the `EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION` branch of `build_context` no longer builds the dense FPS vectors directly over the raw candidate axes (which raised `REPRESENTATION_INCOMPLETE` on the first axis-incomplete frame). It now calls `derive_admissible_sizing_representation(rep.pool, candidate_spec=rep.spec, scope_contract=scope_contract, deployment_claim=objective.claim_scope, id_prefix=run_id)`, builds `vectors = [[float(frame.features[k]) for k in sizing_rep.axes] for frame in rep.pool.frames]` over EVERY frame, and additively caches `sizing_axes_reduced`, `sizing_representation_sha256`, `sizing_adequacy_verdict`, `sizing_per_axis_available_count` alongside the existing keys. Behaviour is otherwise byte-identical to FE-028.
- **tests added / modified (NON-frozen):**
  - `tests/test_fe029_representation_sizing_compatibility.py` (NEW; sha `2c3f26e8…`; 9 tests in 3 classes) —
    - `Fe029NeighbourPbc`: one-atom periodic cubic (a=3.0 → 3.0, axis present); one-atom NON-orthogonal periodic (cell `[[3,0,0],[1,3,0],[0,0,4]]` → matches brute-force shortest-lattice-vector min = 3.0); multi-atom periodic (two atoms 1.1 Å apart in a 20 Å cell → 1.1); non-periodic single atom (→ None; axis absent; NO impute).
    - `Fe029Ffv4cReproduction` (the EXACT ffv4c failure: a saturated eligible pool of periodic single-atom frames + multi-atom periodic frames + the `mean_min_neighbor_distance_A` axis): periodic self-image computed so all 4 PRIMARY axes are universal → `reduced=False`, adequacy PASS, NO `REPRESENTATION_INCOMPLETE`; dense sizing over EVERY frame yields a recommendation via `recommend_labeling_population_sizing`; derivation is deterministic (same axes + spec sha across repeated calls).
    - `Fe029GenuineMissingAxis`: a pool of genuine non-periodic single atoms + multi-atom periodic frames → axes reduced to `ALTERNATIVE_CONTINUOUS_VARIABLES`, `reduced=True`, ALL frames retained, NO impute; an all-single-atom pool with no admissible adequate subset → fail closed with the typed `REPRESENTATION_INSUFFICIENT` gap (no fabricated value, no silent frame dropping).
- **SiO2 as instance only:** none of the FE-029 code encodes any SiO2 (or Si) constant. The periodic-image distance is a pure cell/PBC geometry computation; the sizing-representation derivation is a generic axis-availability audit + adequacy check. The neighbour tests use Cu single/multi-atom synthetic cells; the ffv4c-reproduction and genuine-missing-axis tests use synthetic multi-category manifest pools. The SAME generic code resolves the periodic-single-atom state for arbitrary materials. No element-specific cutoff, no phase name, no per-material human descriptor choice is ever requested.
- **access_control / fail-closed invariants:** (a) uncomputable axes are STILL never imputed — `compute_frame_features` emits only computable axes and `_mean_min_neighbor_distance` returns `None` for a genuinely isolated non-periodic single atom. (b) the sizing derivation NEVER silently drops a frame and NEVER silently removes an axis: every accepted axis is universally present, so the dense matrix keeps every eligible frame, and any axis reduction is admitted ONLY when the reduced representation still PASSes `RepresentationAdequacyEvidence`. (c) when no axis subset is both universally available and scientifically adequate, the path fails closed with the typed `REPRESENTATION_INSUFFICIENT` gap — a framework issue, never a per-material human descriptor request, and never `REPRESENTATION_INCOMPLETE-by-first-frame`. (d) every eligible structure remains accounted for in coverage / provenance / protected-reference exclusion (axis incompleteness never erases a scientific region). (e) nothing binds/executes except the audited FE-026 orchestrator bridge → FROZEN `RunController.bind_new_input`; the existing 3-Judge gate is reused unchanged.

- **c12f_affected:** no.

(1) Fix the PHYSICAL definition first — for periodic structures nearest-neighbour descriptors must account for periodic images; a one-atom periodic primitive cell does NOT have an undefined nearest-neighbour distance (compute the nearest periodic-image distance deterministically via cell/PBC geometry); do NOT add Si-specific logic, element-specific cutoffs, or phase names; add tests for one-atom periodic cubic, one-atom non-orthogonal periodic, multi-atom periodic, and non-periodic single atom (periodic cases must yield finite physically-meaningful distances). ## FE-030 — COSTLY_ACTION_APPROVAL_BOUNDARY + LAZY_ACQUISITION_PLANNING: costly Teacher/GPU compute never dispatches without an explicit action approval, and expensive acquisition planning is deferred past every earlier costly boundary

- **timestamp:** 2026-08-24 session (FRAMEWORK EVOLUTION, executed OUTSIDE any live proof run; post-FE-029).
- **run_context:** no live run touched. The prior fresh run `sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4f` — which demonstrated both defects (a fresh 9,295-frame Teacher baseline dispatched on GPU with `action_approvals={}`, and ~78 min of eager FPS/coverage/population-sizing geometry spent BEFORE the Stage-1 costly boundary on every fresh restart) — is preserved IMMUTABLE as the FE-030 diagnostic run; it was NOT resumed, mutated, re-anchored, or reused. All prior LIVE runs (ffv1/ffv1b/ffv1c + ffv2 + ffv3 + ffv4/ffv4b/ffv4c/ffv4d/ffv4e/ffv4f + C12F + every diagnostic) preserved byte-immutable; `runs/` unmodified. This correction is authored so the NEXT fresh run (`sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4g`) resolves both states safely and cheaply.
- **problem:** two demonstrated defects in the ffv4f fresh run, plus a binding-provenance lesson.
  - **DEFECT 2 (COSTLY_ACTION_APPROVAL_BYPASS, most load-bearing):** `teacher_baseline` declared `approval_boundary: costly_teacher_labeling`, yet real Allegro Teacher forward passes over 9,295 frames executed on GPU while `action_approvals = {}`. The compute boundary had been wrongly relaxed to `None` because the R25 `_CONDITIONALLY_GATED_VALIDATION_ACTIONS` rule read an affirmative "creates no new DFT/protected-reference labels" declaration — a statement about CORPUS GROWTH — as proof that no costly Teacher COMPUTE was incurred. Scientific/downstream-reliance adequacy is orthogonal to permission to spend GPU Teacher inference; it must NOT authorize costly compute.
  - **DEFECT 3 (eager acquisition planning):** `run-campaign` ran the autonomous acquisition planner (whose provider `build_context` performs the expensive FPS / coverage / labeling-population-sizing geometry over the whole candidate pool) BEFORE the stage loop — so it was spent on every fresh restart even when the campaign then paused for human authorization at an EARLIER costly boundary (`teacher_baseline`) and never reached the acquisition stage.
  - **DEFECT 1 (binding-provenance lesson, no code change):** `ORIGINAL_HELDOUT_FIDELITY` was admissible/selected but the fresh recovered-original-holdout reference was supplied only via a stage `reference_yaml` parameter, not registered as a canonical Controller input — so `_protected_reference_from_inputs` (which scans ONLY `controller.state["inputs"]`) could not resolve it. The framework already REQUIRES a controller-bound input; this is an ffv4g authoring correction, guarded by a regression.
- **root_cause:** (Defect 2) `runtimes.pydantic_ai.actions.resolve_action_approval_boundary` relaxed `costly_teacher_labeling` → `None` for `build_teacher_baseline` / `validate_teacher_reference` whenever the proposal declared `dft_labels_used: False` + `protected_reference_labels_used: False`, conflating corpus-growth provenance with compute cost. (Defect 3) `_run_campaign_loop` invoked `plan_acquisition_via_reasoning_roles` + `maybe_install_default_acquisition_provider` in a pre-loop block, before any stage (and thus any costly-action authorization boundary) was reached.
- **change_type:** framework SAFETY + execution-order CORRECTION of two demonstrated defects — NOT a scientific-policy change and NOT an acquisition-science change. NARROW correction ON TOP OF FE-029; the FE-027/FE-028/FE-029 generic representation / sizing / `EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION` / 3-Judge committee / ReplayAgentRuntime / Teacher-first ValidationProfile / RecoveryPlan semantics / frozen scientific policies / 12-stage lifecycle are reused UNCHANGED and were NOT reopened.

## FE-031 — RECOVERED_HOLDOUT_TRANSITIVE_EVIDENCE_NOT_BOUND: a recovered-original-holdout reference's declared structures population is auto-bound as its own hash-verified Controller input at init/bind, so reference_validation evidence is reachable without a manual mid-run bind

- **timestamp:** 2026-08-24 session (FRAMEWORK EVOLUTION, executed OUTSIDE any live proof run; post-FE-030).
- **run_context:** no live run touched. The prior fresh run `sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4g` — which demonstrated the defect (its `recovered-original-holdout` reference.yaml was bound, but the `recovered_original_holdout_test.xyz` structures population it declares was NOT a Controller input, so `reference_validation` fail-closed at its evidence-binding gate until a human ran `bind_new_input(...xyz, copy=False)` mid-run) — is preserved IMMUTABLE as the FE-031 diagnostic run; it was NOT resumed, mutated, re-anchored, or reused. All prior LIVE runs (ffv1/ffv1b/ffv1c + ffv2 + ffv3 + ffv4/ffv4b/ffv4c/ffv4d/ffv4e/ffv4f/ffv4g + C12F + every diagnostic) preserved byte-immutable; `runs/` unmodified. This correction is authored so the NEXT fresh run (`sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4h`) resolves the state autonomously at init, with no manual mid-run binding.
- **problem:** a fresh campaign could bind the `recovered-original-holdout` reference YAML as a Controller input while failing to bind the structures population that reference points at (`structures.path`). The evidence-binding gate then correctly fail-closed: `validate_evidence` rejected the referenced `.xyz` as `evidence is not bound to this run` because the validation-evidence allowlist (`_validation_evidence_allowlist`) is built ONLY from input source/snapshot paths + current/upstream artifacts and does NOT follow `structures.path` inside a YAML. Historically successful runs (r31 precedent) had explicitly bound BOTH files as separate Controller inputs; a fresh authoring that bound only the reference YAML omitted the transitive dependency, and the only remedy in flight was a human noticing the gate rejection and manually calling `bind_new_input`.
- **evidence:** ffv4g `reference_validation` (Stage-2) could not reach its Judges until a human manually bound `recovered_original_holdout_test.xyz`; the DEFECT-1 binding-provenance lesson recorded in FE-030 is the same class (a recovered-original-holdout reference supplied only via a stage parameter / bound only as the YAML, not as the transitively-required structures input). The generic invariant this establishes: for every Controller input of kind `recovered-original-holdout`, the `structures.path` it declares must itself be a canonically hash-bound Controller input BEFORE the run reaches `reference_validation` — with no human mid-run `bind_new_input` required.
- **root_cause:** `workflow.controller` had no mechanism to recognise that a bound reference of an evidence-bearing kind carries a transitive first-class-evidence dependency (its `structures` population). `initialize()` and `bind_new_input()` bound exactly the paths handed to them and never inspected a reference's declared `structures.path`, so the structures file was left outside `controller.state["inputs"]` and therefore outside the evidence allowlist.
- **change_type:** framework AUTHORING-MECHANISM correction (generic fresh-run defect), NOT a scientific-policy change and NOT a relaxation of any evidence gate. NARROW correction ON TOP OF FE-030; the FE-027/FE-028/FE-029/FE-030 generic representation / sizing / `EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION` / costly-action-approval boundary / lazy-acquisition trigger / 3-Judge committee / ReplayAgentRuntime / Teacher-first ValidationProfile / RecoveryPlan semantics / frozen scientific policies / 12-stage lifecycle are reused UNCHANGED and were NOT reopened.
- **files_changed:**
  - `workflow/controller.py` (MODIFIED; **FROZEN file**; `75cd60974315b12c131b69033095226926d9a51acc8f18f01d71d322ddd0173b` → `0f8c5b59f8051e2e2a0bb2a910cab9f4b6c4e3e3be8fc0d90ed67574b821487a`) — three additive edits, no existing behaviour weakened. (1) module-level constant `EVIDENCE_STRUCTURE_REFERENCE_KINDS = frozenset({"recovered-original-holdout"})` + pure helper `referenced_evidence_structure(source, *, project_dir)`: reads a candidate input; returns `None` for anything that is not a YAML reference of a kind in that frozenset declaring a `structures.path` (a plain data/config file, a reference of another kind, unparsable YAML); otherwise resolves the declared structures path (`.format(project_dir=…)`, relative-to-reference-dir), hash-verifies it, and returns `(resolved_path, integrity)`. Fail-closed: a missing structures file raises `FileNotFoundError`; a declared `structures.sha256` that mismatches the file's actual digest raises `ValueError`. (2) `initialize()`: after building `prepared_inputs`, for each prepared input the helper is consulted and, when it returns a structures path not already bound, that path is appended as a `copy=False` hash-bound input (deduplicated against already-declared inputs). (3) `bind_new_input()`: after appending the reference record, the same helper is consulted and the structures file is appended as a `copy=False` input (deduped) with an `input_bound` event carrying `auto_bound_structures_for=<reference>`. This does NOT weaken `validate_evidence` and does NOT make the evidence allowlist recursively trust arbitrary YAML paths — it binds exactly the one declared, hash-verified structures population as an explicit input, identically to a human-authored `copy: false` structures input.
  - `tests/test_architecture_freeze.py` (MODIFIED; frozen-hash SYNC) — the `FROZEN` map's `workflow/controller.py` expected sha256 updated `75cd60974315b12c131b69033095226926d9a51acc8f18f01d71d322ddd0173b` → `0f8c5b59f8051e2e2a0bb2a910cab9f4b6c4e3e3be8fc0d90ed67574b821487a` to bless this correction. All other 23 frozen entries UNCHANGED. **Pin-provenance audit (verified this pass):** the pre-fix `controller.py` was reconstructed by removing exactly the three auto-bind blocks above; its sha256 is `75cd6097…`, byte-identical to the frozen pin recorded at FE-030 close (`controller_matches_freeze = true`). The auto-bind fix is therefore the SOLE, fully-documented delta between the previously-frozen controller.py and `0f8c5b59…` — there is no undocumented drift being blessed by this re-freeze.
- **tests added / modified (NON-frozen):**
  - `tests/test_recovered_holdout_structure_autobind.py` (NEW; sha256 `c3ac2d34a9379ccdb597c2b60876d48002354b9abd196f2df676addef89ee33c`; 6 tests, all hermetic — synthetic empty runs, no SiO2 fixtures, no real Teacher / GPU / network): `test_fresh_init_autobinds_reference_and_structure` (fresh init registers BOTH the reference YAML and the structures population; the structures record is `copy=False`, `snapshot=None`, sha256 recorded from the real file; the structures file is in the reference_validation evidence allowlist; `verify_inputs()` passes; `validate_evidence` does NOT reject the structure as unbound); `test_already_declared_structure_is_not_duplicated` (a pre-declared `copy:false` structures input is not duplicated); `test_missing_structure_fails_closed` (`FileNotFoundError`, run dir not created); `test_structure_hash_mismatch_fails_closed` (`ValueError`, "sha256 mismatch"); `test_unrelated_reference_kind_is_not_autobound` (a `protected-structure-identity` reference declaring a `structures.path` is NOT auto-bound and its structure is still allowlist-rejected by `validate_evidence`); `test_bind_new_input_autobinds_structure_post_init` (post-init bind autobinds the structure and emits the `auto_bound_structures_for` event).
- **SiO2 as instance only:** none of the FE-031 code encodes any SiO2/Si constant, element, phase, population size, or per-material choice. The mechanism is keyed on the generic reference `kind` (`recovered-original-holdout`) only; the set is extended (not per-material logic) if another evidence-bearing reference kind is added. The regressions use synthetic stub structure populations, not SiO2 data.
- **access_control / fail-closed invariants:** (a) `validate_evidence` is UNCHANGED — the evidence allowlist still rejects any path not explicitly bound as a Controller input; the fix binds the one declared+hash-verified structures file as an explicit input, it does not teach the allowlist to follow arbitrary YAML paths. (b) a missing structures file (`FileNotFoundError`) or a declared-sha mismatch (`ValueError`) still fails closed at init/bind and no run directory advances. (c) only references of a kind in `EVIDENCE_STRUCTURE_REFERENCE_KINDS` trigger auto-bind; a reference of any other kind (e.g. `protected-structure-identity`) is untouched and its declared structures remain allowlist-rejected. (d) duplicate bindings are prevented (dedup against already-declared inputs). No costly-action approval, no gate, no Judge, no recovery-taxonomy, no schema_version change.

## FE-032 — STAGE2_REFERENCE_VALIDATION_EVIDENCE_PACKET_INCOMPLETE: the authoritative source→split crosswalk manifest a recovered-original-holdout reference declares is auto-bound as its own hash-verified Controller input and surfaced into the reference_validation lineage join + gate packet, with a pre-costly evidence-readiness preflight that fails closed BEFORE any Teacher/GPU dispatch, and the RootCauseValidationError negated-DFT-prose false positive fixed

This correction is authored so the NEXT fresh run resolves the state autonomously.
- **problem:** the same Stage-2 failure signature appeared in ffv4g AND ffv4h (identical: unanimous 3× REVISE; energy MAE/RMSE 15.73/104.16 meV/atom; force-comp MAE/RMSE 0.156/0.474 eV/Å; 1142 frames / 125233 atoms; **source-split lineage join = 0 joined / 1142 unjoined; resolved domains "unknown"**). The Teacher-vs-DFT numbers were HEALTHY and were NOT the basis of the REVISE — all three mutually-blind Judges REVISEd because the bounded packet did not let them VERIFY the gate criteria: per-frame lineage was missing (0/1142 joined), no split membership resolved, domains "unknown". Because the failure reproduced in a clean post-FE-031 run, it is a GENERIC FRAMEWORK DEFECT, not a run-local recovery problem and not a Teacher scientific-accuracy failure.
- **evidence:** the authoritative split-membership crosswalk manifest `configs/provenance/teacher_training_split_manifest.json` (11424 records `{global_index, source_category, source_local_index, split}`; test=1142) is declared BOTH in the recovered-original-holdout reference.yaml (`split_source_manifest` + `split_source_manifest_sha256`) AND in workflow.yaml (`teacher_evidence_sources.split_source_manifest_paths`, resolved+existence-checked at controller init) — but it was NEVER passed into `bounded_evidence.build_split_crosswalk()`. So the crosswalk saw 0 sources → every one of the 1142 recovered-holdout frames reported `source_split_unjoined` / domain "unknown", even though each frame already carries the exact join keys (`source_category` + `source_local_index`) in its extxyz `atoms.info`. ## FE-033 — STAGE3_ACQUISITION_GATE_EVIDENCE_PACKET_INCOMPLETE: the deterministic parent→sanitized-pool join, deployment-domain mapping (resolved from the closure-bound frozen scope-classification evidence), and selection-control attestations that already exist in the acquisition manifest are surfaced into the acquisition gate's bounded-evidence/gate_outcomes packet so all three mutually-blind Judges can VERIFY the acquisition criteria, mirroring the FE-032 reference_validation surfacer — evidence surfacing only, ZERO frozen files touched

- **timestamp:** 2026-08-24 session (FRAMEWORK EVOLUTION, executed OUTSIDE any live proof run; post-FE-032).
- **run_context:** no live run mutated. The defect was reproduced in the clean post-FE-032 fresh run `sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4k`, preserved IMMUTABLE. ffv4k's Stage-3 `acquisition` gate returned unanimous 3× REVISE despite a valid autonomous AcquisitionPlan (8 selected parents, admissible 108-frame population, EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION geometry-only, `performs_teacher_inference=False`); its gate verdict, Judge votes, AcquisitionPlan, and selected parents are NOT reused as fresh scientific results. All prior LIVE runs (ffv1/ffv1b/ffv1c + ffv2 + ffv3 + ffv4/ffv4b/ffv4c/ffv4d/ffv4e/ffv4f/ffv4g/ffv4h/ffv4i/ffv4j/ffv4k + C12F + every diagnostic) preserved byte-immutable; `runs/` unmodified. This correction is authored so the NEXT fresh run resolves the state autonomously.
- **problem:** ffv4k's Stage-3 `acquisition` gate REVISEd because the bounded gate packet did not let the Judges VERIFY the acquisition criteria: the parent→sanitized-pool join, the per-parent deployment-domain resolution, and the selection-control attestation booleans were all PRESENT in the acquisition manifest (`artifacts/acquisition.manifest.json`) but the generic `_json_summary` dropped them from the packet — so Judges saw selected parents with no surfaced pool-membership proof, no resolved deployment domain (effectively "unknown"), and no explicit `dft_labels_used_as_selection_scores` / `performs_teacher_inference` attestations. This is the exact structural asymmetry FE-032 fixed for `reference_validation`: that stage got a deterministic criterion-evidence surfacer; `acquisition` did not. Because the raw values already exist deterministically at the source, this is a GENERIC EVIDENCE-SURFACING defect, not an AcquisitionPlan scientific-adequacy failure and not a selection-logic problem.
- **evidence:** deterministic offline proof against ffv4k artifacts (no Teacher/GPU): the 8 selected parents (`SiOx_crystal_amorphous_interfaces#{0,10,26,55,64,89,94,100}`) resolve against the hash-bound sanitized pool (`docs/FRESH_SOURCE_BUNDLE_ASSEMBLED_v1/sanitized_pool/sanitized_pool_manifest.json`) to **joined=8, unmatched=0, foreign=0, duplicates=0**, with the declared `sanitized_pool_manifest_sha256` matching the manifest-recorded `pool_manifest_sha256`; each parent's `source_category` (`SiOx_crystal_amorphous_interfaces`) maps via the closure-bound frozen `DeploymentScopeContractV2` label_map to canonical domain `amorphous_SiOx_sub_stoichiometric` (`ClaimRole.PRIMARY_CLAIM`) → **8/8 resolved, 0 unresolved, 0 "unknown"**; and the manifest's `dft_labels_used_as_selection_scores=False` + `performs_teacher_inference=False` are booleans ready to attest. The gate evidence is fully present; the framework simply never surfaced it into the packet.
- **root_cause:** the generic gate-packet builder in `runtimes/pydantic_ai/cli.py` had a stage-specific criterion-evidence surfacer for `reference_validation` (FE-032) but none for `acquisition`, and the generic `_json_summary` compaction elides the manifest's nested join/domain/attestation structure. So the acquisition Judges adjudicated on a packet that did not carry the deterministic acquisition criteria.
- **change_type:** framework EVIDENCE-SURFACING correction (generic Stage-3 defect), NOT a scientific-policy change and NOT a relaxation/weakening of any evidence gate or Judge requirement. NARROW correction ON TOP OF FE-032; the AcquisitionPlan scientific strategy, selected parents, recommended N, FPS sizing, eligibility logic, scientific objective, deployment scope, TeacherValidationPlan logic, the 3-Judge architecture, StageReviewSpecs, closure semantics, and protected-reference policy are ALL reused UNCHANGED and were NOT reopened.

Returns `{"applicable", "ready", "blocking_gaps", "criteria", "pending_execution"}`; when the manifest is not yet materialized it returns a PENDING_EXECUTION record (`ready=True` — nothing blocks a pre-exec geometry-only stage). `ready` is INFORMATIONAL only (Judges adjudicate); the surfacer adds NO preflight fail-close (matching the reference_validation gate-builder pattern and respecting the geometry-only nature of EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION — the costly boundary is downstream `teacher_labeling`). Generic by identity/provenance contract: no material/element/filename/category constant is hardcoded.

No executor, approval boundary, gate verdict, Judge, recovery-taxonomy, or schema_version change.
