## FE-034 — TWELVE-UNIT FRAMEWORK-HARDENING + RELEASE-GATE CONSOLIDATION (canonical Stage 1-12 governance surface hardened; zero-known-blocker release gate; local-provider secret-leak fixed)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-24T ~16:22Z (session file writes; tracked-diff sha256 `36206d29bf34adbf12f4f602f4d6fccb8941861215a555bc50200b5579454433` over git HEAD `0811b14`)

## FE-035 — GENERIC RECOVERY-MATERIALIZATION CONTRACT (ffv4m RECOVERY_EXECUTION_UNVERIFIED dead-loop, pre-acceptance no-op refusal)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-25 (session file writes)
- **run_context:** Generic framework fix OUTSIDE any run, motivated by the real fresh campaign `sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4m` (permanently stopped, byte-immutable; postmortem `docs/postmortems/ffv4m_recovery_execution_unverified.md`). Tasks #557-#562.
- **problem:** A `data_repair` RecoveryPlan dispatched a corrective `action_type = build_data_coverage_report` — the SAME deterministic route action of its return stage (`data_coverage`) — on unchanged inputs. Re-running that executor re-emitted a byte-identical artifact (`DUPLICATE`, sha `6350d02d…` unchanged), which `verify_recovery_execution` correctly rejects as unchanged (`RECOVERY_EXECUTION_UNVERIFIED`, exit 10). There was NO pre-acceptance check that the chosen corrective could ever MATERIALIZE a changed artifact at/downstream of the return stage → an unbreakable `DUPLICATE → RECOVERY_EXECUTION_UNVERIFIED` loop, discovered only after a human approval was spent.
- **evidence:** ffv4m `recovery/recovery-001.json` corrective carried non-empty parameters — `source_report` (pointing at the EXISTING unchanged `data_coverage.json`), `source_report_sha256`, `acquisition_manifest` (existing), and an `required_evidence` LIST OF OPAQUE FREE TEXT the deterministic `build_data_coverage_report` executor has no typed channel to consume. Baseline and regenerated artifact sha256 identical; executor result `DUPLICATE (accepted=false)`. No scientific reacquisition, no bound-plan supersession, no costly downstream action ever ran. This proves the naive signal `corrective.action_type == route_action` is INSUFFICIENT (a same-action corrective CAN legitimately materialize by overriding a real input), and equally that mere presence of corrective parameters is INSUFFICIENT (ffv4m's were opaque free text the executor ignored).
- **root_cause:** No acceptance-time invariant that a corrective action's executor can materialize the state transition recovery-execution verification requires. Materialization through the return stage's OWN route executor happens only via (a) an authorized scientific recompute, (b) supersession of the return stage's bound plan (re-plan on changed inputs), or (c) overriding one of that stage's DECLARED typed route input parameters with a different value; a corrective dispatching a DISTINCT action_type materializes a distinct evidence artifact. ffv4m did NONE of these — its only "change" was undeclared free-text keys — so it was a provable deterministic no-op.
- **change_type:** recovery-contract acceptance-gate (fail-closed) + pure generic classifier + regression (NO scientific-compute change; hash-change verification invariant UNCHANGED and un-weakened)
- **files_changed:**
  - `workflow/recovery_taxonomy.py` (NOT frozen; sha256 `d7ca932194fb26b53687fb881a697db24701e0061ed6fcec51f3bce94e8a33ee`) — added the `MATERIALIZING_TRANSITIONS` vocabulary and the pure, fully generic `classify_recovery_materialization(...)` (names no stage/capability/chemistry/dataset); this pass added the `corrective_supersedes_bound_input` signal so a same-action corrective that overrides a DECLARED typed route input parameter classifies as `input_supersession_replan`, while opaque undeclared keys (ffv4m's `required_evidence`) supersede nothing and classify as a provable no-op (`None`).
  - `workflow/controller.py` (FROZEN; re-froze `47ee90dd…`/`33fd773f…` → `5f510e590caa1f957a09a18c3260c9b7283b22a37f71b6c6b9ff9aac96415a42`) — additive read-only helpers `_workflow_cfg`, `_stage_route_action`, `_stage_route_parameters`, `_return_stage_replans_on_recovery`, and the acceptance guard `_validate_recovery_materialization` wired into `propose_recovery` BEFORE binding. If the corrective classifies as a provable no-op AND the return stage's route action is KNOWN for this run, the plan is refused before approval (never dispatched into the loop); when the route is unknown (a stage with no `pydantic_ai.action` metadata) nothing is rejected and `verify_recovery_execution` stays the backstop — the guard is SOUND to skip, never a false rejection. `start_iteration` refactored to call `_return_stage_replans_on_recovery` (behavior identical). schema_version UNCHANGED at 10.

## FE-036 — GENERIC ACQUISITION SATURATION-CURVE PERFORMANCE HOTFIX (ffv4n Stage-3 uncapped O(n^3) farthest-point stall)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-25 (session file writes)
- **run_context:** Generic framework performance fix OUTSIDE any run, motivated by the real fresh campaign `sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4n` (cleanly stopped during Stage-3 acquisition PLANNING, byte-immutable; diagnostic note `runs/sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4n/DIAGNOSTIC_TERMINATION_NOTE.md`). ffv4n's completed stages are scientifically valid and preserved: `teacher_baseline = NOT_APPLICABLE` (canonical; TVP selected only `ORIGINAL_HELDOUT_FIDELITY`, empty intersection with teacher_baseline capability), `reference_validation = PASS` (3/3 blind Judges, closure-bound gate PASS; global metrics n_frames=1142, n_atoms=125233, energy_MAE=15.7327 meV/atom, energy_RMSE=104.1603, force_component_MAE=0.156127, force_component_RMSE=0.474170). NO AcquisitionPlan was ever produced; ffv4n inherits nothing forward. Classification: OPERATIONAL / FRAMEWORK PERFORMANCE failure, NOT a scientific acquisition failure.
- **problem:** `framework_v2/acquisition/generic_coverage.py::_marginal_novelty_curve` — the deterministic farthest-point marginal-novelty traversal that drives FE-027 §5/§8 saturation/sizing — tracked the chosen set as a Python LIST, so the two per-step `range(n)` membership scans (`if i in chosen` / `if i not in chosen`) were each O(len(chosen)). Overall complexity was **O(n^3)**. FE-027's autonomous acquisition planner (`generic_provider.build_descriptor_space_evidence` → `compute_saturation`) invokes this over the FULL, uncapped candidate pool. On ffv4n's 11,424-frame sanitized pool this is ~1.5×10^12 membership comparisons — single-threaded pure-Python, no persisted progress state, heavily back-loaded — which manifested as a multi-hour Stage-3 planning stall holding a GPU idle.
- **evidence:** Scratch benchmark on a synthetic 11,424-frame 4-axis descriptor space mirroring the sanitized_pool space (n_atoms, number_density, mean_min_neighbor_distance [absent on single-atom frames], max_species_fraction), NO run data / NO GPU: legacy time scales ~7.2–7.6× per doubling of n (confirming cubic) — n=200:0.045s, 400:0.230s, 800:1.667s, 1600:12.651s — extrapolating to **~1.3 h** at n=11,424 (consistent with the observed live stall). The unused `GenericStructuralDescriptorProvider(max_frames_per_category=…)` cap confirms the authors anticipated large-pool bounding but the fallback registration left it uncapped.
- **root_cause:** O(len(chosen)) list-membership tests inside two nested per-step n-loops. The running min-distance was ALREADY carried incrementally; the ONLY superlinear-per-step cost was the list membership. No algorithmic change to the selection logic is required to fix it.
- **change_type:** pure performance optimization, semantics-preserving (NO change to FE-027 sizing semantics; NO approximation, NO subsampling, NO arbitrary cap, NO change to objective/N/distance/axes/scales/knee criterion/tie-breaking; deterministic result BIT-IDENTICAL) + equivalence regression.
- **files_changed:**
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/generic_coverage.py` (NOT frozen; sha256 `0cb6b2367a8ffd980e7638eabad1b854f515ab96716882d37b2a34364d4546ba`) — `_marginal_novelty_curve` rewritten to use an O(1) boolean `chosen_mask` in place of Python-list membership, carrying the same incremental running min-distance. Same seed (index 0), same argmax with strict `>` (lowest-index tie-break), same per-axis missing-axis-aware `_distance` (identical arithmetic and summation order). Complexity **O(n^2 · d)**. Nothing else in the module changed (`_distance`, `_axis_scales`, `compute_saturation`, `_fps_order_and_curve`, sizing all untouched).
  - `tests/test_marginal_novelty_curve_equivalence.py` (NEW; sha256 `f15a13bc9434a90bcb5e0b8c2e53097449dd4408120744dc558d464d7e6bd244`) — embeds a VERBATIM copy of the pre-hotfix O(n^3) traversal as the executable oracle (reusing the module's real `_distance`, so the comparison is BIT-IDENTICAL, not tolerance-based) and instrumented order-exposing legacy+optimized traversals. Asserts identical SELECTED SEQUENCE, curve length (n−1), marginal-novelty values, and knee/saturation across: small hand-checkable 1-D (+ non-unit scale), random seeded × 12 (dims 1–4, n up to ~64), duplicates / all-identical, symmetric ties (lowest-index wins), missing-axis (fixed + random ~30% dropout), single/two-point degenerate, and a 150-point 4-D case. 10 tests, all PASS in 0.34s.
- **behavior_before:** FE-027 Stage-3 saturation-curve planning over an n≈11,424 pool ran O(n^3) (~1.3 h), presenting as an unbounded CPU-bound stall.
- **behavior_after:** identical curve/knee/saturation (bit-for-bit), now O(n^2·d): benchmarked full-pool wall time **39.6 s** (n=200:0.012s → 6400:12.4s scaling ~4× per doubling, confirming quadratic; equivalence re-checked identical at n=1200). Expected ffv4 Stage-3 descriptor-space saturation now completes in tens of seconds, not hours.
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no (deterministic result BIT-IDENTICAL; verified by legacy-oracle equivalence tests)
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no (no run touched; ffv1..ffv4n byte-immutable; ffv4n cleanly stopped, preserved as diagnostic history, NOT resumed/mutated/reinterpreted; its GPU memory released)
- **c12f_affected:** no
- **authorization:** operator directive (this session, verbatim intent): stop the live ffv4n campaign cleanly and preserve it as diagnostic history (operational/framework performance failure, not scientific); fix ONLY the generic performance defect OUTSIDE ffv4n without changing FE-027 sizing semantics; implement the SMALLEST semantics-preserving optimization (O(1) membership mask + incremental running min-distance; retain deterministic order/first point/argmax tie-breaking/distance/curve; O(n^2) or better; no approximation); prove scientific equivalence via legacy-vs-optimized tests; benchmark on the 11,424-frame population with scratch/non-run data; run the full release gate (STOP if any deterministic result changes); make ONE ledger entry + ONE local commit of only fix+tests+ledger (do NOT push; report SHA); only then create fresh run ffv4o (do NOT reuse ffv4n).
- **tests:** `tests/test_marginal_novelty_curve_equivalence.py` (10, all PASS). Targeted acquisition/coverage/FE-027/sizing/FPS/recovery/autonomous suites = 189 passed, 1 skipped. Architecture-freeze + FPS consumers + 12-stage synthetic/closure harness batch = 78 passed.
- **release gate (this session, VERIFIED):** `base` env full suite `python -m pytest tests/ -q` = **1750 passed, 2 failed, 7 skipped** in 61.8s (1740→1750 = exactly the +10 new equivalence tests; ZERO new failures; ZERO deterministic-result changes). The 2 failures are the SAME pre-existing environmental class as FE-034/FE-035 (broken `torchvision` circular import in `base` → `import nequip` fails): `test_stage_d2_c3_adapter::test_build_model_input_edge_index_if_torch_present` and `test_stage_d2_c3_device_consistency::test_preflight_runs_on_cpu_if_torch_present`; unrelated to this change. `tests/test_architecture_freeze.py` = 3 PASS (generic_coverage.py is NOT a frozen file; zero frozen pins touched). **NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0. ZERO code blockers.**
- **test_result:** PASS (release gate green; zero code blockers). NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0.
- **backward_compat:** transparent — the function's signature, return type, and outputs are unchanged and bit-identical; every caller (`compute_saturation`, `compute_target_regime_coverage_inputs`, the autonomous planner) is unaffected except for wall time.

## FE-037 — CANONICAL PROTECTED-POPULATION RESOLVER FOR ACQUISITION (ffv4o Stage-3 planner/executor protected-set inconsistency)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-25 (session file writes)
- **run_context:** Generic framework contract fix OUTSIDE any run, motivated by the real fresh campaign `sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4o` (cleanly stopped by a DETERMINISTIC fail-closed guard during Stage-3 acquisition, byte-immutable; diagnostic note `runs/sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4o/DIAGNOSTIC_TERMINATION_NOTE.md`). ffv4o's completed stages are scientifically valid and preserved: `teacher_baseline = NOT_APPLICABLE` (canonical), `reference_validation = PASS` (3/3 blind Judges, closure-bound gate PASS). The autonomous planner produced an `AcquisitionPlanV2` (`strategy = EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION`, `n_selected = 13`); execution fail-closed BEFORE the Stage-3 Judges, no Stage-4+, no Teacher labeling, no recovery, GPU released. Classification: `FRAMEWORK_CONTRACT_INVARIANT_FAILURE` (capability = acquisition; defect = planner/executor protected-population inconsistency) — NOT a scientific acquisition failure.
- **problem:** The autonomous `EXISTING_POOL_SELECTION` planner (`runtimes/pydantic_ai/default_acquisition_provider.py::_realize_existing_pool`) never loaded the run-bound protected population and emitted a **fabricated, anonymous** `protected_reference_exclusion_report = {"status":"PASS","n_overlaps":0,"protected_excluded_count":<absent>,"reference_id":<absent>}` on the FALSE assumption that the descriptor provider had already removed protected frames. The acquisition EXECUTOR (`executors._validate_existing_pool_plan` → `validation.protected_reference.assert_source_indices_allowed`) independently resolved the REAL protected set and fail-closed: `protected reference leakage through source indices: 2 overlap(s): 3536, 5044`. Two independent, divergent interpretations of the run's protected population — the planner's (empty) and the executor's (authoritative) — is the contract invariant that was violated.
- **evidence:** ffv4o offending plan `runs/sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4o/acquisition/plans/…acquisition_plan.json`: `selected_source_global_indices = [0,3783,3536,4362,26,5025,4724,6525,3353,5044,4930,1349,5308]` (protected seed-pool globals **3536** and **5044** present); `protected_reference_exclusion_report` = anonymous PASS with no `reference_id` / no `protected_excluded_count`. 1142-vs-1143 provenance separately RESOLVED as intentional/correct (one geometry `935e7fb4` appears twice at seed-pool globals 8369+8370 → 1143 protected rows; authoritative `docs/FFV2_PROTECTED_POPULATION_v1/protected_source_indices_1143.txt`); NOT changed by this fix.
- **root_cause:** no single shared authority resolved the run-bound protected population; the planner and executor each hand-rolled their own resolution, and the planner's was a no-op that fabricated a PASS.
- **change_type:** framework contract fix — establish ONE canonical protected-set resolver consumed by BOTH planner and executor, exclude protected rows BEFORE selection/sizing, make the exclusion report authoritative, keep the executor guard as defense in depth. Semantics-preserving for unprotected runs; for protected runs the autonomous N is ALLOWED to change (protected rows are legitimately removed before sizing).
- **files_changed:**
  - `validation/protected_reference.py` (sha256 `4bb1d9b568403f4dc09ce5daa0903c5d6b358ce1d30a74890fbf1ab3d2163b88`) — NEW `resolve_protected_population(reference_yaml)`: THE ONE canonical resolution (thin wrapper over `validate_reference_config`) returning `{reference_id, reference_path, protected_source_indices (sorted unique), protected_source_rows}`; fails closed on ambiguous/incomplete lineage, never a silent empty set. `assert_source_indices_allowed` unchanged (kept as the executor's independent guard).
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/cli.py` (sha256 `e4a1c5be543766328fdbac10961f7a74d4c00057368a876c08f82728433c9ab7`) — NEW `_acquisition_protection_reference_yaml(controller)`: resolves WHICH reference.yaml the `acquire_structures` executor enforces (mirrors `_proposal_from_stage` acquire_structures resolution exactly), so the planner excludes against the SAME reference the executor re-checks; returns None only for an explicitly unprotected campaign.
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/default_acquisition_provider.py` (sha256 `45a4352fcec6650594e8aa2d65af7e1b4bfa4d7ced5419763319e73600563baa`) — NEW static `_resolve_protected(controller)` → `(reference_id, {protected globals})` via the canonical resolver. `_realize_existing_pool` now EXCLUDES the canonically-resolved protected rows from the eligible pool BEFORE descriptor/FPS selection and marginal-novelty sizing (fails closed `EMPTY_ELIGIBLE_POOL` if nothing admissible/non-protected remains), records the REAL `protected_excluded_count`, and its disjointness checker INDEPENDENTLY recomputes overlap (raises `PROTECTED_EXCLUSION_REGRESSION` on any hit — never a fabricated PASS). The generation path (LOCAL_PERTURBATION / TEACHER_DRIVEN_MD / STRUCTURE_GENERATION) now resolves the same canonical set and fails closed `PROTECTED_PARENT_SELECTED` if any selected source-global parent is protected.
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/plan_assembly.py` (sha256 `b9c74bda05a1f1dc3e0fd869ee432ad23824764638d74ae00066a6dcf7db26e5`) — `build_existing_pool_projection` / `build_legacy_projection` accept `protected_reference_id`, `protected_candidate_count`, `protected_excluded_count`, `eligible_population_after_exclusion` and write them into an AUTHORITATIVE exclusion report (names the reference; real counts).
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/executors.py` (sha256 `1d9862abe53d3840b0917b2ae4a0cdb0e7d87e058abdb356e73a960071f7cb6b`) — `_validate_existing_pool_plan` and `_validate_acquisition_plan` resolve the protected set through the SAME `resolve_protected_population`, keep the independent `assert_source_indices_allowed` guard, and now REJECT an anonymous exclusion report (missing `reference_id`) or one naming a different reference than the run binds — an anonymous PASS can no longer masquerade as authoritative.
  - `tests/test_ffv4o_protected_exclusion.py` (NEW; sha256 `9b62b1dfcbca19a4bd0afc76ba4fed67d89c8d8d439e0d9bb603b401be4db053`) — pins the exact ffv4o failure (3536/5044 rejected by the executor guard), planner==executor set parity, the REAL `_realize_existing_pool` excluding protected rows before FPS/sizing with the autonomous N changing when a protected row was the FPS knee (essential), authoritative-vs-anonymous/mismatched report handling, and edge cases (no protected, one protected, all-protected → fail closed, out-of-range protected ignored, LOCAL_PERTURBATION + TEACHER_DRIVEN_MD protected-parent fail-closed). 13 tests, all PASS.
- **behavior_before:** the autonomous existing-pool planner could select protected seed-pool rows and emit an anonymous fabricated PASS exclusion report; only the executor's independent guard caught the leak (fail-closed at Stage-3, wasting the planning pass).
- **behavior_after:** the planner EXCLUDES the canonically-resolved protected rows before selection/sizing (autonomous N may change), the exclusion report is authoritative (names the reference, real counts), an anonymous/mismatched report is rejected at bind time, the executor guard remains as defense in depth and now always finds zero overlap, and every generation backend refuses a protected parent. Planner-resolved protected set == executor-resolved protected set BY CONSTRUCTION (both call `resolve_protected_population`).
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no (unprotected runs bit-identical; for protected runs the change is a legitimate deterministic protected exclusion the framework was always required to perform)
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no (no run touched; ffv1..ffv4o byte-immutable; ffv4o cleanly stopped, preserved as diagnostic history via an ADDITIVE note, NOT resumed/mutated/reinterpreted; GPU released)
- **c12f_affected:** no
- **authorization:** operator directive (this session, verbatim intent): retire ffv4o as diagnostic history (framework contract invariant failure, not scientific); resolve the 1142-vs-1143 provenance before changing code; establish ONE canonical protected-set resolver consumed by BOTH planner and executor; exclude the protected population BEFORE descriptor/FPS selection and sizing (do not generate-then-delete, do not hand-preserve N); make the exclusion report authoritative and independently recomputed before bind; KEEP the executor guard as defense in depth; regression-test the exact 3536/5044 failure + edge cases incl. N-changes; cross-check every acquisition backend; run the full release gate proving planner==executor and FE-036 O(n^2) intact; make ONE ledger entry + ONE local commit of only fix+tests+ledger (do NOT push; report SHA); only then create fresh run ffv4p (do NOT reuse ffv4o).
- **tests:** `tests/test_ffv4o_protected_exclusion.py` (13, all PASS). Targeted protected/acquisition/executor/existing-pool/contract suites = 77 passed, 1 skipped. FE-036 equivalence + generic_coverage + FE-029 sizing + architecture-freeze = 27 passed.
- **release gate (this session, VERIFIED):** `base` env full suite `python -m pytest tests/ -q` = **1763 passed, 2 failed, 7 skipped** in 65.4s (1750→1763 = exactly the +13 new ffv4o regression tests; ZERO new failures). The 2 failures are the SAME pre-existing environmental class as FE-034/FE-035/FE-036 (broken `torchvision` circular import in `base`): `test_stage_d2_c3_adapter::test_build_model_input_edge_index_if_torch_present` and `test_stage_d2_c3_device_consistency::test_preflight_runs_on_cpu_if_torch_present`; unrelated to this change. Planner==executor protected-set equality PROVEN (`ResolverParityTests::test_planner_and_executor_resolve_identical_set`). FE-036 O(n^2) re-benchmarked intact on an 11,424-frame pool: `_fps_order_and_curve` ~3.6× per doubling (quadratic, not the ~8× of cubic), full sizing 2.5 s. **NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0. ZERO code blockers.**
- **test_result:** PASS (release gate green; zero code blockers). NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0.

## FE-038 — TYPED DATA-COVERAGE ASSESSABILITY CONTRACT + CAPABILITY-AWARE RECOVERY ACCEPTANCE (ffv4p Stage-4 assessability/recovery capability mismatch)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-25 (session file writes)
- **run_context:** Generic framework contract fix OUTSIDE any run, motivated by the real fresh campaign `sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4p` (RETIRED as byte-immutable diagnostic history; note `runs/sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4p/DIAGNOSTIC_TERMINATION_NOTE.md`). ffv4p's science was SOUND: Stage 1 `teacher_baseline` 3/3 PASS, Stage 2 `reference_validation` 3/3 PASS (fresh Teacher-vs-DFT over 1142 TEST frames: energy_mae=15.732701 meV/atom, force_component_mae=0.15612691 eV/Å, 0 failed), Stage 3 `acquisition` 3/3 PASS with FE-037 validated live (protected_candidate_count=1143, protected_excluded_count=15, eligible_after=93, autonomous N=8, indices [0,100,55,26,10,64,94,89], 0 overlaps). Classification: `FRAMEWORK_CAPABILITY_CONTRACT_GAP` subclass `DATA_COVERAGE_ASSESSABILITY_AND_RECOVERY_CAPABILITY_MISMATCH` — NOT a demonstrated acquisition insufficiency.
- **problem:** Two Stage-4-boundary capability gaps. (1) **Assessability gap:** `build_data_coverage_report` could only emit a single `config_type_coverage` dimension and a coarse `COMPLETE/PARTIAL/NOT_ASSESSABLE` `coverage_status`; `coverage_status` fell to `PARTIAL` purely because no frozen `coverage_requirement` existed — the ABSENCE of a criterion was reported as a soft-negative rather than as `NOT_ASSESSABLE`. It could not enumerate declared deployment dimensions, mark unsupported dimensions NOT_ASSESSABLE per-dimension, or surface FE-037 protection + acquisition-lineage equality as typed evidence. (2) **Recovery capability gap:** the autonomous RecoveryPlan's `corrective_action=build_data_coverage_report` carried the required fixes as OPAQUE free-text `requirements=[...]` superseding no declared typed route input; it was accepted on prose, then the deterministic materialization guard correctly refused to bind it at `propose_recovery` time (re-run of the return-stage route action on unchanged inputs → byte-identical artifact → `RECOVERY_EXECUTION_UNVERIFIED`, campaign exit 2). Acceptance did not verify, BEFORE dispatch, that the selected corrective action could materialize the required evidence.
- **root_cause:** (1) the framework had a coverage-criterion CONSUMPTION path (a frozen `coverage_requirement.min_frames_by_config_type`) but no honest tri-state assessability contract and no per-dimension typed record, so absence-of-criterion and criterion-failure were conflated into `PARTIAL`. (2) capability-aware validation of a RecoveryPlan happened only implicitly, at bind time, in the controller's no-op guard — never at acceptance, so an unmaterializable plan was accepted on prose and only rejected after dispatch.
- **change_type:** framework contract fix — (a) a typed data-coverage assessability contract with three honest outcomes and never-fabricate invariants; (b) a capability-aware recovery-acceptance layer that runs the SAME materialization taxonomy the controller backstop uses, but at acceptance, BEFORE binding. The controller's no-op materialization guard (FE-035) is preserved UNCHANGED as the backstop. No arbitrary constant (min N, min frames per class, class quota, percentage) is introduced; where no frozen/derivable criterion exists the dimension is honestly NOT_ASSESSABLE.

Also enforces acquisition-lineage equality_result consistency and protected-reference `result == PASS iff post_selection_overlap_count == 0`.

## FE-039 — GAP-BASED STAGE-4 CONFIGURATION-SPACE ADEQUACY GATE + TARGETED AUTONOMOUS REACQUISITION (ffv4p N=8 = 7-of-8 declared classes zero-occupancy)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-25 (session file writes)
- **run_context:** Generic framework capability build OUTSIDE any run, motivated by the same RETIRED, byte-immutable `sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4p` diagnostic history that motivated FE-038. No run is created, resumed, mutated, or reinterpreted; ffv4p's immutable acquisition plan (`selected_source_global_indices=[0,100,55,26,10,64,94,89]`, all `config_type=SiOx_crystal_amorphous_interfaces`) is read only as a regression fixture.
- **design_decision (operator, fixed this session):** Stage 3 = autonomous INITIAL acquisition proposal (the FE-036 FPS/marginal-novelty knee is a STARTING proposal; its N is never an accepted adequacy result). Stage 4 = INDEPENDENT configuration-space adequacy gate against the FROZEN deployment scope. Stage 4 failure = targeted autonomous REACQUISITION (return_stage=acquisition, not data_coverage). Stages 8–11 = final model/deployment adequacy (Student accuracy / deployment fidelity / MD stability / physical-observable fidelity), which this gate explicitly does NOT claim.
- **problem:** FE-038 made per-declared-structure-class support honestly NOT_ASSESSABLE because no per-class support metric existed. But a genuinely INDEPENDENT, invented-constant-free support signal DOES exist: the frozen, human-authored `config_type -> canonical structure-class` `label_map` carried by the deployment scope. Without consuming it, Stage 4 could not detect that ffv4p's N=8 acquired population (all one `config_type`) structurally covers only ONE of the 8 declared deployment structure classes, leaving 7 with zero acquired representatives — a real, evidence-derived configuration-space gap, not a fabricated one.
- **root_cause:** the framework consumed the frozen `label_map` for the acquisition-gate deployment-domain MAPPING evidence (`acquisition_readiness`, FE-033) but never folded per-`config_type` acquired occupancy back through it into a per-declared-class PRESENCE assessment at Stage 4, and had no deterministic routing from a zero-occupancy declared class to a targeted reacquisition that widens source eligibility.
- **change_type:** additive framework capability. Declared-class OCCUPANCY via the frozen `label_map` is the ONLY support signal; a class with zero acquired representatives is UNSUPPORTED — a DEFINITIONAL presence/absence fact (`MIN_STRUCTURAL_OCCUPANCY = 1`), explicitly NOT an invented minimum-N, per-class quota, percentage, or descriptor-distance threshold. When no frozen `label_map` is bound, per-class support stays NOT_ASSESSABLE (FE-038 behavior preserved). Representative deployment STATE POINTS (named thermodynamic points without atomic coordinates) are NOT turned into structure classes and are never given a fabricated structural embedding/PASS.
- **files_changed:**
  - `validation/coverage_gap_assessment.py` (NEW; sha256 `69116f842c23f317f81da622359b447ece0e460a579f17a833a368cb584ffe7b`) — pure deterministic gap module. Hard-codes NO SiO2-x class name, frame count, or material constant; reasons only over the frozen `label_map`'s own entries + observed per-`config_type` acquired counts. `build_label_index` (fail-closed on a malformed entry or a duplicate `raw_label` disagreeing on `canonical_domain`); `resolve_config_type_domain` (real class → `(canonical_domain, claim_role)`; else `(None, reason)` for `unmapped`/`out_of_scope_diagnostic`/`ambiguous` — none credits a class; sentinels `OUT_OF_SCOPE_DOMAIN=OUT_OF_SCOPE_DIAGNOSTIC`, `AMBIGUOUS_DOMAIN=AMBIGUOUS`); `compute_structure_class_occupancy` (fold counts into per-class occupancy, bucketing unmapped/out-of-scope/ambiguous/mapped-outside-declared separately); `build_structure_class_dimensions` (one FE-038 typed dimension per declared class: `frozen_deployment_domain` PRESENCE criterion `{kind:structural_presence, min_occupancy:1, observed_occupancy:n, met:n>=1}` → PASS/FAIL; NOT_ASSESSABLE when no `label_map`); `unsupported_structure_classes` (extract FAIL classes from the SAME dimension records the verdict aggregated, so recovery names exactly what the gate found); `derive_reacquisition_targets` (per unsupported class, the pool `config_type` families the frozen `label_map` maps to it that still have pool candidates, optionally `primary_claim`-only; a class with none is `unremediable` — a genuine scientific boundary; `widened_eligible_source_categories` = the NEW families beyond already-eligible; `materializable` iff ≥1 new family — INVENTS no frame count and selects no frame identity; sizing/selection remain the acquisition machinery's autonomous job over the widened, protected-excluded, already-acquired-excluded pool).

NEW `_resolve_frozen_structure_class_label_map` resolves the frozen map provenance-faithfully: (1) inline `deployment_domain.structure_class_label_map`, else (2) a bound `DeploymentScopeContractV2` JSON at proposal `scope_classification_evidence_path` (the same `{raw_label,canonical_domain,claim_role}` map `acquisition_readiness` resolves); invents no mapping. `_exec_build_data_coverage_report` then, on a COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT typed verdict with structurally-unsupported classes, names each zero-occupancy class as a concrete `identified_gaps` entry and never lets legacy `coverage_status` assert COMPLETE over an unsupported region (downgrades to PARTIAL).
  - `tests/test_fe039_coverage_gap_gate.py` (NEW; sha256 `a3d6680a47712147cf09a3729c630b23b911cbdea135023e8495e4450378af58`) — 21 tests, all PASS. Primary regression drives the ACTUAL ffv4p immutable counts + the real frozen `label_map`: 7 of 8 declared classes zero-occupancy → COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT, the one occupied class (`amorphous_SiOx_sub_stoichiometric`, occupancy 8) PASS, all dims `frozen_deployment_domain` provenance, full typed block re-validates under the FE-038 invariants, recovery targets widen+materialize; plus the no-false-failure guard (all-classes-occupied → SUFFICIENT, so N=8 failure is DERIVED not hard-coded). Section-9 axes: zero-occupancy → INSUFFICIENT; all supported → no false failure; absent `label_map` → NOT_ASSESSABLE never a silent PASS; out-of-scope/ambiguous/unmapped never credit a class; inconsistent frozen map fails closed; cumulative population flips an unsupported class to supported (preserving iter0 frames); targets name only families (no frame identity, no size key); no-pool-candidate → unremediable boundary; all-already-eligible → not materializable (cannot dispatch byte-identical); primary_claim_only restricts widening; recovery reroute-to-reacquisition-replan accepted while a no-op data_coverage re-run is rejected before dispatch with the FE-035 controller guard preserved as backstop.
- **behavior_before:** every DECLARED deployment structure class was reported NOT_ASSESSABLE (no per-class support metric), so a population covering only 1 of 8 declared classes surfaced no configuration-space insufficiency and no targeted-reacquisition route.
- **behavior_after:** when the run carries the frozen `label_map`, Stage 4 emits a `frozen_deployment_domain` PRESENCE dimension per declared class (PASS iff ≥1 acquired frame maps to it, else FAIL=UNSUPPORTED); the report-level tri-state is DERIVED; a zero-occupancy class is named as a concrete gap and is deterministically routable to a targeted reacquisition that widens source eligibility to exactly the pool families the frozen map ties to the unsupported classes, superseding the prior plan (a class with no remaining pool candidate is an honest `unremediable` boundary). No `label_map` → NOT_ASSESSABLE, unchanged.

On that axis the immutable ffv4p N=8 is COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT (7 of 8 declared classes have zero acquired representatives), every gap remediable from the pool — a configuration-space adequacy result, NOT a Stage-8–11 model-fidelity result.

## FE-041 — TYPED `representative_geometry_only` DATA-COVERAGE ACCESS MODE + AUTHORITATIVE FROZEN-INPUT PROPAGATION (ffv4q Stage-4 access-provenance expressivity gap)
- **timestamp:** 2026-08-25 (session file writes)
- **run_context:** Narrow framework expressivity fix triggered by the ffv4q autonomous campaign (`runs/sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4q`), now RETIRED (`DIAGNOSTIC_TERMINATION_NOTE.md`, classification `FRAMEWORK_CONTRACT_EXPRESSIVITY_GAP` / subclass `DATA_COVERAGE_TEACHER_ACCESS_GEOMETRY_ONLY_UNREPRESENTABLE`). ffv4q is byte-immutable and NOT resumed or mutated; its fresh TVP, Stage-1 NOT_APPLICABLE, Stage-2 reference_validation PASS (1142 frames, E-MAE 15.7326 meV/atom), Stage-3 acquisition PASS (N=13), Stage-4 report + four demonstrated unsupported deployment classes, 3/3 REVISE, rejected recovery, and all approvals are preserved.
- **problem:** The frozen scientific inputs truthfully declare `teacher_training_data_access: representative_geometry_only` (Teacher training data available only as representative GEOMETRIES; zero DFT labels), but the deterministic data-coverage schema (`ACCESS_MODES = {full, representative, unavailable}`) could not represent it, and the executor read the value from the LLM proposal, defaulting to `representative`. The truthful geometry-only provenance was silently collapsed to `representative`, and all three mutually-blind Stage-4 Judges REVISEd on that provenance ambiguity; the recovery plan (re-run the same route action on unchanged inputs) was then correctly rejected as non-materializable → campaign `exit_code=2`.
- **root_cause:** two coupled expressivity gaps: (1) the access-mode enum lacked a term for label-free representative-geometry access; (2) `_exec_build_data_coverage_report` sourced `teacher_training_data_access` from the proposal (`p.get(..., "representative")`) rather than from the authoritative frozen run-bound input (the bound `dataset_policy`, which declares the true value), so an LLM/default value could override frozen scientific provenance.
- **change_type:** minimal schema + propagation fix ONLY. NO Stage-4 redesign, NO change to FE-039 coverage semantics, NO change to acquisition/recovery algorithms, NO new threshold, NO material-specific path or constant. Adds one typed access mode using the exact authoritative vocabulary and resolves the value from frozen inputs; fails closed on conflict.
- **files_changed:**
  - `validation/data_coverage.py` (sha256 `dae4acd766f39f9e8d92e30e2d5c467d33483387a84f4662b63730ba7d0f85bc`) — `ACCESS_MODES` gains `representative_geometry_only` (now `{full, representative, representative_geometry_only, unavailable}`). The `access == "unavailable"` → NOT_ASSESSABLE invariant and every other validator check are unchanged; geometry-only is an assessable mode (geometries exist), so it does NOT force NOT_ASSESSABLE.

Every frozen source that declares the field must agree exactly; distinct values raise `TEACHER_ACCESS_CONFLICT` (fail closed). When no frozen source declares it, it falls back to the historical proposal-parameter path and finally the historical default `representative` (preserving pre-FE-041 behavior for existing callers). `_exec_build_data_coverage_report` now calls this resolver instead of `p.get("teacher_training_data_access", "representative")`, and emits the resolved value plus a new additive `teacher_training_data_access_provenance` field (resolved_from, resolved_value, and per-source {source_role, source_path, source_sha256, value}). NEVER collapses `representative_geometry_only`→`representative` and NEVER upgrades it to `full`. FE-039 gap gate, protection provenance, and lineage-equality blocks are untouched, so the four structurally unsupported classes still surface.
  - `tests/test_fe041_geometry_only_access_provenance.py` (NEW; sha256 `7a07dc5806646b9866ad1c56f7eee4d8bf49eb5a19835b599eb20469f25f89c2`) — 12 tests (directive regressions A–G), all PASS. ## FE-042 — deterministic Stage-4 coverage-adequacy gate control (COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT blocks Stage-5 even when all Judges PASS)

- **change_id:** FE-042
- **problem:** the three mutually-blind Stage-4 Judges adjudicate the HONESTY of the data-coverage report (lineage, provenance, exclusion, assessability), not its scientific ADEQUACY. On the immutable ffv4r run the report was honest and all three Judges returned PASS, so the gate recorded PASS and advanced to Stage-5 (`teacher_labeling`) even though `coverage_assessment.assessment_status == "COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT"` with seven declared deployment structure classes carrying zero acquired representatives. A costly downstream stage thus became reachable over configuration space the campaign had itself derived as unsupported.
- **root_cause:** no deterministic control tied Stage-4 progression to the FE-038/FE-039 coverage-adequacy verdict; adequacy lived only in the report and was never enforced at the gate. The recomputed gate verdict in `_validate_vote_bundle` is strictly a function of the three Judge votes, so an insufficient-but-honest report cannot be blocked through the vote bundle — the control must be a verdict downgrade inside `record_gate`.
- **change_type:** deterministic gate control (additive verdict downgrade; no coverage/acquisition science, no threshold, no schema, no Judge-prompt change).
- **files_changed:**
  - `workflow/controller.py` (sha256 `11aa89390906353c83120908886482e593e9cca7e42f210be9ee528be13e7579`, was `5f510e590caa1f957a09a18c3260c9b7283b22a37f71b6c6b9ff9aac96415a42`) — in `record_gate`, after verdict validation and before any PASS-only precondition block, a would-be `PASS` on a stage whose registered artifact reports `coverage_assessment.assessment_status == "COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT"` is downgraded to `REVISE` via the new `_coverage_adequacy_control(name)` helper. The helper reads the stage's registered artifact(s), and when a report is INSUFFICIENT returns a typed record (`control=fe042_coverage_adequacy`, `assessment_status`, `unsupported_structure_classes` via FE-039 `unsupported_structure_classes(dimensions)`, `recommended_return_stage="acquisition"`, `report_path`, `report_sha256`). The record is attached to the gate event and to `pending_recovery` (recommended_return_stage="acquisition"). All existing PASS-only guards gate on `verdict == "PASS"`, so post-downgrade they are correctly skipped; a Judge REVISE/FAIL is untouched; PASS on a SUFFICIENT/NOT_ASSESSABLE/absent report is untouched. Additive `+54` lines.
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/cli.py` (NOT a frozen file; `+6` lines) — `_propose_recovery_via_reasoning_roles` surfaces `pending.get("coverage_adequacy")` into the recovery evidence packet iff the deterministic control (not the Judges) forced the REVISE, so the autonomous RecoveryPlan targets `return_stage=acquisition` on the exact unsupported regions.
  - `tests/test_architecture_freeze.py` (`+30` lines) — `FROZEN["workflow/controller.py"]` re-frozen to the new sha256 above and a v28 FREEZE_REVISION narrative appended disclosing the additive `record_gate` downgrade branch + `_coverage_adequacy_control` helper (schema_version UNCHANGED; `cli.py` remains not-frozen).
  - `tests/test_fe042_coverage_adequacy_gate_control.py` (NEW) — the 3 required regressions: (1) Judge PASS + INSUFFICIENT → gate REVISE, `pending_recovery` bound, `coverage_adequacy.recommended_return_stage=="acquisition"`, `_ensure_no_pending_recovery` raises (Stage-5 unreachable); (2) Judge PASS + SUFFICIENT → PASS, no `pending_recovery`, Stage-5 eligible; (3) replay of the immutable ffv4r `artifacts/data_coverage.json` fixture routes REVISE with exactly the seven unsupported classes and matching `report_sha256`/`report_path`.
- **behavior_before:** an honest but scientifically insufficient Stage-4 report with 3/3 Judge PASS recorded a gate PASS and made Stage-5 (`teacher_labeling`, a costly boundary) reachable over unsupported configuration space.
- **behavior_after:** a would-be Stage-4 PASS on a `COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT` report is deterministically downgraded to REVISE, binds `pending_recovery` with `recommended_return_stage="acquisition"`, and blocks Stage-5 until coverage is sufficient. The REVISE routes an autonomous RecoveryPlan to targeted reacquisition over the exact FE-039 unsupported classes; cumulative population then triggers Stage-4 reassessment. Honest-PASS-on-sufficient and every Judge REVISE/FAIL path are unchanged.
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no (gate-control only; no numeric scientific artifact computed or recomputed; no run re-executed).
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no (ffv1..ffv4r byte-immutable; ffv4o/ffv4p/ffv4q RETIRED; ffv4r's `artifacts/data_coverage.json` used read-only as the test-3 fixture; GPU untouched).
- **c12f_affected:** no
- **authorization:** operator directive (this session, verbatim intent): fix ONLY the live ffv4r defect so that `COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT` deterministically prevents Stage-5 progression and triggers a scientific REVISE → `return_stage=acquisition` even when all Stage-4 evidence-quality Judges return PASS; no coverage/acquisition science, threshold, schema, or unrelated-component change; exactly three required regressions; run directly-affected tests + the normal full regression once; ONE ledger entry + ONE local commit; NO push.
- **tests:** `tests/test_fe042_coverage_adequacy_gate_control.py` (3, all PASS); directly-affected gate `tests/test_architecture_freeze.py` (3 PASS) + Stage-4 coverage/recovery suites (`tests/test_fe038_coverage_assessability.py`, `tests/test_fe039_coverage_gap_gate.py`, `tests/test_data_coverage_v2.py`, `tests/test_recovery_reacquisition_unit4.py`) green.
- **release gate (this session, VERIFIED):** full suite = **1789 passed, 20 failed, 7 skipped** (1786 + 3 new FE-042 tests). The 20 failures are the SAME pre-existing `r4-runtime` environmental class present in the pre-patch baseline (verified by `git stash` baseline comparison: broken torchvision/nequip/lammps wiring, PRODUCER_CONTEXT_BUDGET_EXCEEDED, full-suite ordering pollution) — identical failure set before and after the patch. **NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0. ZERO code blockers.**
- **test_result:** PASS (release gate green; zero code blockers). NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0.
- **backward_compat:** additive — the downgrade fires only on a would-be PASS whose registered Stage report is `COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT`; SUFFICIENT/NOT_ASSESSABLE/absent reports and all Judge REVISE/FAIL paths are unchanged, as are every other gate precondition and the FE-035..FE-041 guards.
- **prospective_only:** yes (validated by the fresh successor run ffv4s; ffv4r preserved as the pre-fix diagnostic and is not resumed).

## FE-043 — recovery→acquisition wiring: an approved `return_stage=acquisition` recovery re-enters the canonical Stage-3 planner to bind a fresh superseding AcquisitionPlan before `acquire_structures` (ffv4s reacquisition-dispatch gap)

- **change_id:** FE-043
- **problem:** on the live ffv4s run, FE-042 correctly downgraded a 3/3-Judge-PASS-on-`COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT` Stage-4 gate to REVISE and bound an autonomous RecoveryPlan with `return_stage=acquisition` and `recovery_context.corrective_action=acquire_structures` targeting the five zero-occupancy declared classes. On resume the recovery corrective dispatch fail-closed: `recovery corrective action dispatch failed: INVALID: PLAN_INPUT_REQUIRED: AcquisitionPlan is required before acquire_structures execution` (executor_failed, campaign_failed, exit 2, acquisition stage). The FE-042/FE-039 targeted-reacquisition loop was thus not materializable end-to-end.
- **root_cause:** in `_run_campaign_loop`, the corrective-dispatch branch (`iteration.recovery_execution.status=="required"` + `recovery_context.corrective_action` present + `return_stage` not completed) called `_dispatch_recovery_corrective_action(... acquire_structures ...)` and `continue`d BEFORE the forward loop's normal lazy-planner branch (`_stage_route_action==acquire_structures` → `maybe_install_default_acquisition_provider` + `plan_acquisition_via_reasoning_roles`). `start_iteration` had already, and correctly, SUPERSEDED the stale bound plan so a coverage-deficit re-acquisition re-plans (instead of regenerating byte-identical candidates), so at dispatch time NO AcquisitionPlan was bound — and nothing re-entered the canonical planner to bind a fresh one. `dispatch.authorize_and_execute` then fail-closed on `acquisition_plan_sha256_from_proposal` (the correct fail-closed outcome only when no autonomous planner exists; ffv4s HAS the FE-027 default planner, so it must re-plan).
- **change_type:** minimal wiring fix (additive; re-enters the SINGLE canonical Stage-3 acquisition planner — no second recovery-specific planner, no acquisition/recovery redesign, no new scientific criteria/threshold/policy/planner).
- **files_changed:**
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/cli.py` (NOT a frozen file; `+26` lines) — in `_run_campaign_loop`'s recovery corrective-dispatch branch, before `_dispatch_recovery_corrective_action`, a guard: when `_stage_route_action(c, return_stage) == "acquire_structures"`, re-enter the SAME canonical lazy-planner path the forward loop uses — `maybe_install_default_acquisition_provider(c)` then `plan_acquisition_via_reasoning_roles(...)` — so the production Stage-3 planner reads this run's already-bound FE-042/FE-039 recovery evidence + cumulative acquired population and binds a fresh, coverage-gap-targeted superseding AcquisitionPlan, which the corrective dispatch then executes `acquire_structures` against. Returns the planner's own paused/terminal result when it yields one; otherwise reloads the controller and proceeds to the unchanged dispatch. Idempotent: once a plan is bound the provider's `applies()` is False, so a resumed/re-looped recovery iteration re-plans nothing. NO change to `workflow/controller.py` (frozen) → NO `tests/test_architecture_freeze.py` re-freeze required.
  - `tests/test_fe043_recovery_reacquisition_wiring.py` (NEW) — the five required regressions driven through the real `run_campaign`/`_dispatch_recovery_corrective_action`/`dispatch.authorize_and_execute` path (canonical planner + `acquire_structures` executor substituted by observable fixtures, the same pattern as `tests/test_run_campaign_recovery.py`): (A) a `return_stage=acquisition` recovery whose re-entered planner binds no plan cannot dispatch `acquire_structures` — it fail-closes `PLAN_INPUT_REQUIRED`; (B) the single canonical acquisition-planning path (provider install + `plan_acquisition_via_reasoning_roles`) is what the recovery re-enters, in order, before dispatch, while the stale plan is already retired; (C) the exact ffv4s fixture (five unsupported classes) binds a fresh uniquely-active superseding plan, retires the pre-recovery plan, and dispatches without `PLAN_INPUT_REQUIRED`; (D) `start_iteration` retires ONLY the stale acquisition plan, preserving the cumulative already-acquired population and other inputs for the planner to exclude-from / build upon; (E) after the corrective reacquisition the acquisition stage is completed against the enlarged cumulative population and Stage-4 (`data_coverage`) is the next stage eligible to reassess it.
- **behavior_before:** an approved `return_stage=acquisition` recovery ran `acquire_structures` with no bound plan and fail-closed `INVALID: PLAN_INPUT_REQUIRED` (exit 2, campaign_failed at acquisition); the autonomous FE-042/FE-039 reacquisition loop could not complete.
- **behavior_after:** the same recovery re-enters the canonical Stage-3 planner, binds a fresh superseding coverage-gap-targeted AcquisitionPlan, and the corrective `acquire_structures` dispatch executes against it; the cumulative population enlarges and Stage-4 becomes eligible for reassessment (the first reacquisition is NOT required to immediately achieve COVERAGE_SUFFICIENT). Normal forward-path acquisition and every non-acquisition recovery path are unchanged.
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no (control-plane wiring only; no numeric scientific artifact computed or recomputed; no run re-executed).
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no (ffv1..ffv4r byte-immutable; ffv4o/ffv4p/ffv4q RETIRED; ffv4s preserved as the pre-fix diagnostic record and is NOT resumed; GPU untouched).
- **c12f_affected:** no
- **authorization:** operator directive FE-043 (this session, verbatim intent): implement ONE minimal recovery→acquisition wiring fix so a `return_stage=acquisition` recovery re-enters the existing production Stage-3 acquisition planner (fresh superseding plan → plan binding → `acquire_structures`) instead of dispatching plan-less; do NOT redesign acquisition or recovery, add no new planner/criteria/thresholds/policies, do not require the first reacquisition to reach SUFFICIENT; use the immutable ffv4s failure as the primary fixture; five required tests; run directly-affected tests + the normal full regression once; ONE ledger entry + ONE local commit; NO push; do NOT launch ffv4t.
- **tests:** `tests/test_fe043_recovery_reacquisition_wiring.py` (5, all PASS); directly-affected recovery/reacquisition suites `tests/test_recovery_reacquisition_unit4.py` + `tests/test_run_campaign_recovery.py` green.
- **release gate (this session, VERIFIED):** full suite `python -m pytest tests/ -q --continue-on-collection-errors` = **1829 passed, 2 failed, 7 skipped, 1 collection error** (includes the 5 new FE-043 tests). The 2 failures (`test_stage_d2_c3_adapter::test_build_model_input_edge_index_if_torch_present`, `test_stage_d2_c3_device_consistency::test_preflight_runs_on_cpu_if_torch_present`) are the pre-existing torch/`torchmetrics` environmental class (both `if_torch_present`-gated, broken `torchmetrics.functional` import chain). The 1 collection error (`tests/test_fe042_coverage_adequacy_gate_control.py`) is a pre-existing environment shadowing: an installed `~/.local/.../site-packages/tests/__init__.py` package shadows the repo's `tests/` namespace, so that file's `from tests.test_full_lifecycle_integration import ...` cannot resolve here — unrelated to FE-043 and untouched by it (FE-043's own test uses the bare `test_full_lifecycle_integration` import, like the majority convention). None involve the changed `cli.py` path; every recovery/campaign suite exercising `_run_campaign_loop` passed. **NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0. ZERO code blockers.**
- **test_result:** PASS (release gate green; zero code blockers). NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0.
- **backward_compat:** additive — the re-plan guard fires only inside the recovery corrective-dispatch branch for a `return_stage` whose route action is `acquire_structures`; every other recovery route action, the normal forward acquisition path, and all FE-035..FE-042 guards are unchanged.
- **prospective_only:** yes (the fix makes the FE-042/FE-039 reacquisition loop materializable; ffv4s preserved as the pre-fix diagnostic and is not resumed; a fresh successor is NOT launched this session).

## FE-044 — recovery corrective `acquire_structures` resolves its approval boundary through the SAME typed-effect binding as the forward path (ffv4t spurious `costly_teacher_labeling` gate)

- **change_id:** FE-044
- **problem:** on the live ffv4t run (fresh successor on FE-043 `94a06f9`), FE-042 forced a Stage-4 REVISE on `COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT` and FE-043 correctly re-entered the canonical Stage-3 planner and bound a fresh superseding **existing-pool-selection** AcquisitionPlan (input 044, sha `5f4c87f3…`; `acquisition_plan_bound` emitted, PLAN_INPUT_REQUIRED closed). The recovery corrective dispatch of `acquire_structures` then fail-closed `APPROVAL_REQUIRED: action requires approval: costly_teacher_labeling plan_sha256=5f4c87f3…` (executor_failed, campaign_failed, exit 2, acquisition stage) — even though the byte-identical geometry-only selection had run on the forward path (campaign_4) with the boundary relaxed to `None` and NO approval.
- **root_cause:** forward and recovery dispatch resolved the `costly_teacher_labeling` boundary ASYMMETRICALLY. The forward path (`run_production_stage`, `cli.py`) calls `_proposal_from_stage` THEN `_bind_acquisition_plan_for_stage`, which for an existing-pool plan sets `performs_teacher_inference=False` (selection runs no Teacher); `actions.resolve_action_approval_boundary("acquire_structures", "costly_teacher_labeling", params)` then relaxes the boundary to `None` via `_incurs_teacher_inference_effect`. `_dispatch_recovery_corrective_action` rebuilt its proposal from `_proposal_from_stage` + the approved `recovery_context.corrective_action.parameters` but NEVER ran it through `_bind_acquisition_plan_for_stage`, so no `performs_teacher_inference` effect flag was injected; the boundary check fail-closed (missing flag ⇒ effect assumed incurred) and kept `costly_teacher_labeling`. The bound plan identity flowed through correctly (the failure carries the fresh plan sha) — only the typed-effect classification was missing. This is an approval-routing wiring bug, not a genuine costly boundary (the identical action is Teacher-free on the forward path).
- **change_type:** minimal wiring fix (additive; routes the corrective acquisition proposal through the SINGLE canonical typed-effect binder the forward path already uses — no new classifier, no hard-coded recipe/effect, no acquisition/recovery redesign, no new criteria/threshold/policy; the bound AcquisitionPlan stays authoritative).
- **files_changed:**
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/cli.py` (NOT a frozen file) — in `_dispatch_recovery_corrective_action`, immediately after assembling the corrective `proposal` and before `dispatch_via_controller`, route it through `proposal = _bind_acquisition_plan_for_stage(c, proposal)` (a no-op for every non-`acquire_structures` corrective action). ## FE-045 — `verify_inputs` skips the SOURCE-byte re-check for SUPERSEDED input records (mirroring `active_inputs()`); snapshot integrity stays mandatory for every input (ffv4t-eng1 superseded-shared-source re-gate blocker)

- **change_id:** FE-045
- **problem:** on the FE-044 engineering continuation ffv4t-eng1 (re-anchored to FE-044 `eb977f2`), the geometry-only corrective `acquire_structures` EXECUTED successfully (11 existing-pool frames), then the forward loop re-selected the acquisition stage (gate not yet PASS) and `run_production_stage → verify_inputs` (`cli.py`) RAISED `RuntimeError: declared workflow input changed after initialization: …/acquisition/plans/…acquisition_plan.json` → campaign_failed (EXIT_VALIDATION_REJECTED). The corrective reacquisition worked but the immediately-following acquisition re-gate could not proceed.
- **root_cause:** the superseded AcquisitionPlan input 043 and its superseding plan input 044 legitimately share ONE mutable source path (`acquisition/plans/<run_id>.acquisition_plan.json` — the canonical Stage-3 planner writes a fixed filename), so binding the superseding plan overwrote that source with 044's bytes (`5f4c87f3…`). 043's historical bytes (`a3fb3109…`) survive only in its immutable content-addressed snapshot (`inputs/043-…`), which still verifies. But `verify_inputs` iterated `self.state["inputs"]` — ALL inputs including superseded — and called `verify_artifact(source, source_integrity)` for each, so the redundant SOURCE re-check of provenance-only 043 (recorded `a3fb3109…` vs on-disk `5f4c87f3…`) turned a legitimate supersession into a false "declared workflow input changed" invariant failure. The framework already models the active/superseded split via `active_inputs()` (controller.py:797); `verify_inputs` was the one integrity path that did not honor it. On ffv4t this was masked because the earlier FE-044 approval failure stopped the run before any `verify_inputs`.
- **change_type:** minimal integrity-scoping fix (additive guard; no new schema/threshold/policy; no science, acquisition, coverage, or recovery logic touched).
- **files_changed:**
  - `workflow/controller.py` (FROZEN — re-frozen) — in `verify_inputs`, the per-input SOURCE re-check (`verify_artifact(source, source_integrity)`, which raises `declared workflow input changed after initialization`) is now gated behind `if not record.get("superseded")`, mirroring `active_inputs()`. The per-input SNAPSHOT integrity check above the gate is UNCHANGED and still fail-closes EVERY input (superseded or active) against its immutable content-addressed snapshot, and the run-level `code_revision` guard is untouched. Source-byte equality remains mandatory and fail-closed for every ACTIVE input. `tests/test_architecture_freeze.py` re-frozen `11aa8939…` → `2cb69859af792d5d7007c9808abd1ad7a8da53c0883055f7718fd9d0ebb5fac0` with a disclosed FREEZE_REVISION paragraph.
  - `tests/test_fe045_verify_inputs_superseded_source.py` (NEW) — five required regressions over the REAL `RunController.verify_inputs` (genuine `bind_new_input` / `supersede_bound_acquisition_plan` lifecycle): (1) a superseded plan whose shared source was overwritten by the superseding plan, with an intact frozen snapshot, verifies WITHOUT error; (2) an ACTIVE input whose source bytes change still fails closed `declared workflow input changed after initialization`; (3) a SUPERSEDED input whose snapshot is corrupted still fails closed `run input snapshot integrity check failed`; (4) the ACTIVE superseding plan is still fully source-verified (intact → passes, tampered → fails closed); (5) the exact ffv4t-eng1 043/044 input records (superseded 043 sharing the plans/ source path now holding active 044's bytes) verify WITHOUT stopping on 043.
- **behavior_before:** after a `return_stage=acquisition` recovery superseded a stale AcquisitionPlan and bound a fresh superseding plan to the SAME shared source path, the next `verify_inputs` (acquisition re-gate) fail-closed `declared workflow input changed after initialization` on the superseded record's overwritten shared source — blocking the loop immediately after the corrective reacquisition executed.
- **behavior_after:** `verify_inputs` verifies the immutable snapshot of every input (superseded or active) and re-checks the mutable SOURCE bytes only for ACTIVE inputs; a superseded input whose shared source was legitimately overwritten by its superseding plan no longer trips the changed-input invariant, while every active input's source and every input's snapshot remain fail-closed. Frozen-provenance integrity is fully preserved (historical bytes live on in the snapshot, still verified).
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no (input-verification integrity scoping only; no numeric scientific artifact computed or recomputed; no run re-executed).
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no (ffv1..ffv4t and ffv4t-eng1 byte-immutable; ffv4t-eng1 preserved as the pre-fix diagnostic record and is NOT resumed under a different code revision; GPU untouched).
- **c12f_affected:** no
- **authorization:** operator directive FE-045 (this session, verbatim intent): implement ONLY the science-neutral input-verification fix — for records with `superseded == True` verify their immutable snapshot/provenance but do NOT require their shared mutable source path to still contain their historical bytes; keep source verification mandatory and fail-closed for ACTIVE inputs; do not weaken snapshot verification; use the existing active/superseded semantics rather than a material-specific exception; five focused tests; run directly-affected tests + the normal full regression once; ONE ledger entry + ONE local commit; NO push; do NOT launch a fresh Stage-1 campaign, do not audit or redesign, do NOT modify framework code beyond FE-045.
- **tests:** `tests/test_fe045_verify_inputs_superseded_source.py` (5, all PASS); `tests/test_architecture_freeze.py` green (re-freeze applied).
- **release gate (this session, VERIFIED):** full suite `python -m pytest tests/ -q --continue-on-collection-errors` = **1839 passed, 2 failed, 7 skipped, 1 collection error** (includes the 5 new FE-045 tests). The 2 failures (`test_stage_d2_c3_adapter::test_build_model_input_edge_index_if_torch_present`, `test_stage_d2_c3_device_consistency::test_preflight_runs_on_cpu_if_torch_present`) are the pre-existing torch/`torchmetrics` environmental class; the 1 collection error (`tests/test_fe042_coverage_adequacy_gate_control.py`) is the pre-existing `tests/`-namespace `from tests.test_full_lifecycle_integration import …` shadowing — both unrelated to FE-045 and identical to the FE-044 baseline (passed count rose 1834→1839 = the 5 new FE-045 tests). **NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0. ZERO code blockers.**
- **test_result:** PASS (release gate green; zero code blockers). NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0.
- **backward_compat:** additive — the new guard only skips the SOURCE re-check for records already flagged `superseded`; every active input's source verification, every input's snapshot verification, the run-level `code_revision` guard, and all FE-035..FE-044 guards are unchanged.
- **prospective_only:** yes (the fix unblocks the acquisition re-gate that follows a corrective reacquisition; ffv4t and ffv4t-eng1 preserved as immutable diagnostics and not resumed; work continues via a provenance-explicit engineering continuation ffv4t-eng2, not a fresh Stage-1 run).

## FE-046 — targeted coverage-gap reacquisition is CUMULATIVE (accumulate prior-accepted frames, never supersede-and-drop) + per-declared-class occupancy FLOOR, and each recovery cycle must STRICTLY reduce the unsupported set or fail closed (ffv4t-eng2 non-convergent-reacquisition boundary)

- **change_id:** FE-046
- **problem:** on the FE-045 engineering continuation ffv4t-eng2 (parent ffv4t-eng1, `747eeee`), the Stage-4 coverage-gap recovery loop ran end-to-end for the first time and EXPOSED a structural non-convergence. A `return_stage=acquisition` recovery re-enters the canonical Stage-3 planner (FE-043), which binds a FRESH SUPERSEDING AcquisitionPlan; that plan re-sized from scratch via a GLOBAL farthest-point marginal-novelty knee (~11 frames) over a re-narrowed eligible set, with NO per-declared-class occupancy floor and NO accumulation of the prior-accepted candidates. Consequence: the ~11 global-FPS frames concentrated in the dense/most-novel families so only ~3 of the 8 declared SiO2-x deployment structure classes got an occupant per cycle, and WHICH classes were covered OSCILLATED. Observed on ffv4t-eng2: plan 043 covered 4/8 (unsupported = liquid_or_melt, surface, oxygen_vacancy, condensed_pure_Si_boundary); recovery #1 plan 044 covered 3/8 (gained surface_SiO2, LOST amorphous_bulk_SiO2 + high_pressure_SiO2) — coverage REGRESSED 4/8→3/8. The pool was NOT exhausted, and `recovery_policy` is null (no loop ceiling), so blindly resuming would spin hosted-provider LLM diagnosis/proposal credits with no convergence.
- **root_cause:** the existing-pool reacquisition selection (a) SUPERSEDED-and-replaced the prior candidate set rather than accumulating it (already-accepted frames/classes were silently dropped), and (b) selected purely by global-FPS novelty knee with no per-declared-class occupancy floor — so the framework's OWN Stage-4 coverage contract (per-declared-class occupancy≥1 via the frozen `config_type→structure-class` label_map, FE-039) was not honored by its selection/sizing. Compounding this, nothing enforced monotonic progress across recovery cycles, so an oscillating/regressing reacquisition could loop uncapped.
- **change_type:** targeted acquisition-selection + recovery-progress fix (additive; no new scientific numeric artifact; no material name / count / ratio hard-coded; deterministic remediation driven by the FROZEN Stage-4 label_map, not an LLM scope escape). No frozen framework file touched (`controller.py` UNCHANGED — no freeze re-anchor).
- **files_changed:**
  - `framework_v2/acquisition/selection.py` — new `select_candidates_from_indices(...)`: build a `CandidateSelectionResult` from an EXPLICIT, already-ordered index list (dedup preserving first-seen order) instead of re-deriving indices by FPS, so the caller can compose `prior_accepted_union + per_unsupported_class_occupancy_floor + fps_topup`. The MANDATORY protected-reference disjointness check still applies and still fails closed (the `CandidateSelectionResult` validator rejects a non-PASS report), so an explicit composition can never bypass the protected-reference invariant.
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/default_acquisition_provider.py` — INVARIANT 1 (cumulative + monotonic). New detection + composition inside `_realize_existing_pool`, keyed off PERSISTED facts so it survives stage invalidation: `_latest_coverage_gap_unsupported` (reads the most recent Stage-4 `coverage_adequacy` gate event's unsupported set; `None` ⇒ INITIAL acquisition, legacy path untouched); `_prior_accepted_source_globals` (UNIONs `selected_source_global_indices` across all superseded `acquisition_plan` inputs = the cumulative acquired population). When BOTH are present, the block loads the run's frozen scope label_map (`_load_scope_classification_evidence`), calls `derive_reacquisition_targets`, and **fails closed IMMEDIATELY** (`AcquisitionCapabilityGap` `POOL_LACKS_STRUCTURE_CLASS`) if ANY unsupported class is unremediable from the pool (user-directed boundary: no partial-progress-then-fail). Otherwise it widens the admissible families to keep every prior-accepted frame in scope + make each floor family selectable, then `_compose_cumulative_coverage_selection` builds `cumulative_prior_accepted (retain all; fail closed CUMULATIVE_FRAME_DROPPED if any prior frame is no longer eligible) + per-uncovered-class occupancy floor (≥1 marginal-novelty frame each; fail closed POOL_LACKS_STRUCTURE_CLASS if a family is empty) + marginal-novelty FPS top-up to max(knee_k, core)`, dispatched via `select_candidates_from_indices`. The composed set can only GROW relative to the cumulative+floor core, so a shrinking knee can never evict an accepted frame or a covered class.
  - `runtimes/pydantic_ai/cli.py` — INVARIANT 2 (strict-reduction-or-fail-closed). New `_fe046_recovery_progress_check(controller, failed_stage, current_cov)`: compares the current coverage-gap cycle's unsupported declared-class set against the most recent DISTINCT prior `coverage_adequacy` gate event (different `report_sha256`; both read from persisted events, so the ceiling survives stage invalidation). Returns `None` on the first cycle or on a strict subset (genuine reduction), else a fail-closed `CampaignRunResult(CAMPAIGN_FAILED, EXIT_BLOCKED_POLICY, "RECOVERY_NO_PROGRESS: …")`. Wired into `_propose_recovery_via_reasoning_roles` right after `failed_stage` is read, so a non-shrinking (or oscillating) reacquisition fails closed BEFORE any paid Analyst/Orchestrator dispatch (emits a `recovery_no_progress` event).
  - `tests/test_fe046_cumulative_monotonic_reacquisition.py` (NEW) — 17 focused regressions across five classes: (A) `select_candidates_from_indices` explicit-index build + order-preserving dedup + fail-closed on non-PASS disjointness + out-of-range rejection; (B) cumulative-floor composition retains the prior-accepted core, floors every uncovered unsupported class, grows only via knee top-up, lets accumulation alone cover a class (no redundant floor frame), and fails closed on an unremediable family (`POOL_LACKS_STRUCTURE_CLASS`) or a dropped prior frame (`CUMULATIVE_FRAME_DROPPED`); (C) persisted-fact detection helpers (latest coverage gate + superseded-plan global union, excluding active/non-plan records); (D) realize-level IMMEDIATE unremediable fail-closed over a real `_realize_existing_pool` call; (E) the invariant-2 progress gate — first cycle proceeds, strict reduction proceeds, no-progress + oscillation fail closed `EXIT_BLOCKED_POLICY`, same-`report_sha256` dedup compares against the earlier distinct cycle.
- **behavior_before:** a `return_stage=acquisition` recovery re-selected a fresh ~knee-sized global-FPS set that SUPERSEDED the prior candidates with NO per-class occupancy floor — coverage oscillated/regressed (4/8→3/8 on ffv4t-eng2, gaining surface_SiO2 while losing amorphous_bulk_SiO2 + high_pressure_SiO2) and, with `recovery_policy` null, could loop uncapped.
- **behavior_after:** a coverage-gap reacquisition ACCUMULATES every prior-accepted frame and adds a per-declared-class occupancy floor so all still-unsupported (remediable) classes are covered in ONE cycle (coverage monotonic by construction); an unremediable class fails closed immediately with a typed scientific-boundary gap; and any recovery cycle that does not strictly shrink the unsupported set fails closed before any paid LLM dispatch instead of looping.
- **scientific_compute_changed:** no (acquisition-selection composition + recovery-gate control only; no Teacher/Student numeric artifact computed or recomputed; no run re-executed; ffv4t-eng2 preserved as the immutable pre-fix diagnostic and NOT resumed).
- **existing_sci_artifacts_changed:** no (ffv1..ffv4t, ffv4t-eng1, ffv4t-eng2 byte-immutable; GPU untouched).
- **c12f_affected:** no
- **authorization:** operator directive FE-046 (this session, verbatim intent): (1) "Recovery acquisition is cumulative and monotonic — 이미 accepted된 frames/classes를 버리지 않는다"; (2) "Every successful recovery iteration must strictly reduce the unsupported-class set. 줄이지 못하면 또 무한 반복하지 말고 fail closed." Plus the explicit follow-up decision that an UNREMEDIABLE unsupported class (pool has no source family the frozen label_map maps to it) must "Fail closed immediately" (no partial-progress-then-fail). This directive supersedes the FE-045 "do NOT redesign acquisition/recovery" scope for exactly these two invariants.
- **tests:** `tests/test_fe046_cumulative_monotonic_reacquisition.py` (17, all PASS).
- **release gate (this session, VERIFIED):** full suite `python -m pytest tests/ -q --continue-on-collection-errors` = **1856 passed, 2 failed, 7 skipped, 1 collection error** (includes the 17 new FE-046 tests). The 2 failures (`test_stage_d2_c3_adapter::test_build_model_input_edge_index_if_torch_present`, `test_stage_d2_c3_device_consistency::test_preflight_runs_on_cpu_if_torch_present`) are the pre-existing torch/`torchmetrics` environmental class; the 1 collection error (`tests/test_fe042_coverage_adequacy_gate_control.py`) is the pre-existing `tests/`-namespace `from tests.test_full_lifecycle_integration import …` shadowing — both unrelated to FE-046 and identical to the FE-045 baseline (passed count rose 1839→1856 = the 17 new FE-046 tests). No frozen file changed (`tests/test_architecture_freeze.py` green, no re-anchor). **NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0. ZERO code blockers.**
- **test_result:** PASS (release gate green; zero code blockers). NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0.
- **backward_compat:** additive — the cumulative/floor path fires ONLY when a prior superseded acquisition_plan AND a coverage-gap gate both exist (a targeted reacquisition); INITIAL acquisition (`fe046 is None`) runs the unchanged pre-FE-046 FPS-to-knee selection. The invariant-2 gate fires ONLY for a `coverage_adequacy` recovery. No frozen file touched; all FE-035..FE-045 guards are unchanged.
- **prospective_only:** yes (the fix makes the FE-042/FE-043/FE-044/FE-045 reacquisition loop convergent + capped; ffv4t / ffv4t-eng1 / ffv4t-eng2 preserved as immutable diagnostics and NOT resumed under a different code revision; any continuation is a provenance-explicit fresh run, human-authorized separately).
- **status:** VERIFIED (release gate green). ffv4t / ffv4t-eng1 / ffv4t-eng2 NOT resumed; NO push.

### FE-046-AMEND — unsupported-class remediation is DOMAIN-LOCAL AUTONOMOUS SIZING (per-class FPS saturation), not one guaranteed frame; presence (occupancy>0) is kept SEPARATE from adequacy (class-local novelty saturation)

- **change_id:** FE-046-AMEND (amends FE-046; same two invariants, only the unsupported-domain *sizing semantics* change)
- **problem:** FE-046 as first committed guaranteed exactly ONE marginally-novel frame per still-uncovered class (an occupancy FLOOR) and then did a single GLOBAL FPS top-up to the pool-wide knee. That conflates **structural presence** (Stage-4 occupancy≥1) with **sampling adequacy** (how many frames a domain actually needs): a class became PRESENT at its first frame and was then only incidentally grown by a *global* diversity step, so a large/diverse unsupported domain could be under-sampled to a single representative. Stage-4 occupancy>0 may keep defining PRESENCE, but it must NOT by itself set the number of recovery frames for a domain.
- **fix (only `_compose_cumulative_coverage_selection` sizing semantics; cumulative-union + strict-progress logic unchanged):** for every currently unsupported AND remediable class, resolve its admissible source families from the frozen label_map, build that class's OWN candidate population (protected frames already excluded from `item_ids`; already-acquired frames excluded via `sel_set`), and run the SAME generic `recommend_labeling_population_sizing` (FPS marginal-novelty saturation knee) **WITHIN THAT CLASS ONLY**. The class-local `recommended_population_size` (never a human N, never a fixed per-class quota, never a percentage) sets how many NEW frames that class contributes; the class-local FPS `selected_positions[:k]` are the picks. Union every class-local addition with the cumulative accepted population. The pool-wide FPS top-up REMAINS only as an OPTIONAL SECONDARY diversity step AFTER all classes have independently satisfied their class-local sizing (`max(knee_k, len(selected))` — grows, never substitutes, never drops the core). New provenance fields `domain_local_added_by_class` / `n_domain_local_frames` record the per-class autonomous counts; `floor_added`/`n_floor_added` retained as per-class presence markers for back-compat. Threaded `coverage_gap_sha256` + `sizing_id_prefix` into the `fe046` dict so each class-local sizing emits a typed, gap-anchored `LabelingPopulationSizingEvidence`.
- **files (all UNFROZEN — no re-anchor):** `runtimes/pydantic_ai/default_acquisition_provider.py` (`_compose_cumulative_coverage_selection` floor→domain-local sizing; `fe046` dict gains `coverage_gap_sha256`+`sizing_id_prefix`); `tests/test_fe046_cumulative_monotonic_reacquisition.py` (+6 tests, Class F: A diverse pool→>1; B classes produce different N; C class-local sizing independent across classes; D count is data-driven not a fixed quota; E FE-046 cumulative/monotonic invariants intact; F real ffv4t-eng2 gap classes get multi-frame domain-local populations).
- **preserved FE-046 invariants:** (1) cumulative population never shrinks; (2) previously-supported domains cannot disappear; (3) post-recovery unsupported set must strictly shrink (invariant-2 gate unchanged); (4) unremediable class → fail closed immediately (`POOL_LACKS_STRUCTURE_CLASS`); (5) protected overlap stays zero (selection disjointness validator unchanged).
- **authorization:** operator directive (this session, verbatim intent): "Keep the existing FE-046 cumulative-union and strict-progress logic. Modify ONLY the unsupported-domain sizing semantics" → domain-local autonomous sizing; "No human N. No fixed per-class quota. No percentages. No material-specific counts." Global top-up allowed only as optional secondary.
- **release gate (this session, VERIFIED):** full suite `python -m pytest tests/ -q --continue-on-collection-errors` = **1862 passed, 2 failed, 7 skipped, 1 collection error** (the 2 torch failures + fe042 collection error are the identical pre-existing environmental baseline; passed count rose 1856→1862 = the 6 new amendment tests). No frozen file changed. **NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0.**
- **predicted per-domain additional counts on the ffv4t-eng2 faithful fixture** (structured synthetic descriptors — per-family clusters, real primary-claim families + sanitized_pool sizes; real run-time counts emerge from the real descriptor knee at eng3): `liquid_or_melt_SiO2` → 9 (family `liquid`, 313), `oxygen_vacancy_SiO2` → 7 (`vacancy` 278 + `vacancy_int_AL` 780), `condensed_pure_Si_boundary` → 6 (`silicon_bulk_amo` 159 + `silicon_crystalline_main` 1257 + `silicon_liquid` 76 + `silicon_surfaces` 214 + `silicon_defects` 423). Each is a data-driven multi-frame population, NOT one-by-construction.
- **status:** VERIFIED (release gate green). ffv4t / ffv4t-eng1 / ffv4t-eng2 NOT resumed; eng3 NOT launched; NO push.
