### FE-047 — the FE-046 cumulative-recovery projection must carry the COMPOSED-population sizing evidence (recommended_population_size == final_n_selected), not the INITIAL pool-wide global knee k

- **change_id:** FE-047 (minimal consistency fix on top of FE-046-AMEND; NOT an acquisition-science change)
- **problem:** the FE-046 cumulative branch composes the selection as `cumulative_prior UNION per-class domain-local saturation UNION optional global top-up` (`final_n_selected`), but the existing-pool projection was still attached the pool-wide INITIAL `sizing` record whose `recommended_population_size == global knee k`. The deterministic plan validators require `recommended_population_size == len(selected_source_global_indices)` (`framework_v2/acquisition/validators.py:210-213` plan-proposal path; `runtimes/pydantic_ai/executors.py:1471-1474` `_validate_existing_pool_plan` bind/execute path). Whenever `cumulative_prior + domain_local > k` (i.e. `final_n > k`, the normal cumulative-recovery case) the composed-yet-self-consistent plan was REJECTED on all three deterministic retries → campaign_failed exit 2. This is exactly the ffv4t-eng3 exit-2 the diagnostic run exposed; it is a sizing-EVIDENCE consistency defect, not an LLM/scientific error.
- **fix (only the existing-pool projection's sizing evidence; ZERO acquisition-science change):** in `_realize_existing_pool`, when the FE-046 cumulative branch fired (`composition is not None`) derive the projection's sizing via new static helper `_fe046_projection_sizing`, which `model_copy(update=...)`s the pool-wide sizing to `recommended_population_size = final_n` and `selected_positions = ordered_local` (the actual composed selection). The pool-wide global knee k, each unsupported class's domain-local knee, cumulative-prior count, domain-local additions, global top-up count, and final_n are ALL preserved as provenance in the sizing `rationale` (and, independently, in the selection_result's `composition`); k is NEVER relabeled as the final recommendation. `_compose_cumulative_coverage_selection` was enriched to record `domain_local_knee_by_class`, `global_pool_knee_k`, `n_global_topup`, and `final_n_selected` in the composition dict so the helper reads composed facts, not re-derived ones. The helper fails closed (`AcquisitionCapabilityGap`, `gap_kind="CUMULATIVE_SIZING_INCONSISTENT"`) if `final_n_selected` / `|ordered_local|` / `final_n` disagree, so an inconsistent sizing can never be emitted. The INITIAL branch (`composition is None`) is byte-untouched: `pool_sizing = sizing`.
- **UNCHANGED (per authorization scope):** cumulative accepted frames; unsupported-class set; domain-local FPS/saturation sizing; class-local knees; selected frame identities; protected-reference exclusion; optional global top-up behavior; FE-046 monotonic/progress semantics; all initial/non-recovery acquisition.
- **files (all UNFROZEN — no re-anchor):** `runtimes/pydantic_ai/default_acquisition_provider.py` (`_compose_cumulative_coverage_selection` composition-dict enrichment; new `_fe046_projection_sizing` static helper; `_realize_existing_pool` projection now selects composed sizing for the cumulative branch); `tests/test_fe046_cumulative_monotonic_reacquisition.py` (+5 tests, Class `TestFE047CumulativeSizingEvidenceConsistency`: (1)+(2) `recommended==final_n` and `final_n>global_k` valid in recovery; (3) global knee k + per-class domain-local knees preserved as provenance, input sizing object not mutated; (4)+(5) selected frame identities and domain-local knees/additions unchanged by FE-047; (6) fail-closed `CUMULATIVE_SIZING_INCONSISTENT` when the composed count disagrees; (7) the real `_validate_existing_pool_plan` ACCEPTS the FE-047 projection and REJECTS the pre-fix global-k projection with the exact live `recommended_population_size must equal n_selected` error). Proof (6-authorization: initial/non-recovery unchanged) is structural — `composition is None` → `pool_sizing = sizing` untouched — and covered by the suite's unchanged initial-acquisition tests.
- **authorization:** operator directive FE-047 (this session, verbatim): "AUTHORIZED — FE-047 minimal consistency fix … For the FE-046 cumulative branch: final_n_selected = size(cumulative_prior UNION domain_local_saturation_additions UNION optional_global_topup). The AcquisitionPlan sizing evidence must therefore satisfy: recommended_population_size == final_n_selected … Do NOT discard the original sizing information. Preserve separately in the sizing rationale/provenance … original pool-wide global knee k; cumulative prior count; each unsupported class's domain-local knee / added N; optional global top-up count; final_n_selected. Do NOT relabel the old global knee k as though it were the final recommendation." Explicitly NOT an acquisition-science change.
- **release gate (this session, VERIFIED):** full suite `python -m pytest tests/ -q --continue-on-collection-errors` = **1867 passed, 2 failed, 7 skipped, 1 collection error** (the 2 torch/`torchmetrics` failures `test_stage_d2_c3_adapter` / `test_stage_d2_c3_device_consistency` + the `tests/test_fe042_coverage_adequacy_gate_control.py` `tests.`-namespace collection error are the identical pre-existing environmental baseline; passed count rose 1862→1867 = the 5 new FE-047 tests). No frozen file changed (no re-anchor). **NEW_REGRESSIONS_FROM_THIS_CHANGE = 0.**
- **backward_compat:** additive/consistency-only — the composed sizing is emitted ONLY on the FE-046 cumulative-recovery branch (`composition is not None`); INITIAL acquisition and every FE-035..FE-046 guard are byte-unchanged. ffv4t / ffv4t-eng1 / ffv4t-eng2 / ffv4t-eng3 preserved immutably (eng3 is the diagnostic run that exposed the defect; NOT re-anchored or mutated).
- **status:** VERIFIED (release gate green). eng4 continuation (`sio2-sox-allegro-simplenn-ffv4t-eng4`, parent eng3, reason `FE047_CUMULATIVE_SIZING_EVIDENCE_MISMATCH`) launched under this commit; NO push.
